def validate_system_C1() -> None:
    """Validate system."""
    global ws_flags_C1
    if ws_file_statuses_C1.ws_cust_status != '00':
        print("ERROR: CUSTOMER FILE OPEN FAILED")
        ws_flags_C1.ws_error_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_file_statuses_C1.ws_acct_status != '00':
        print("ERROR: ACCOUNT FILE OPEN FAILED")
        ws_flags_C1.ws_error_flag = 'Y'

def process_deposits_C1() -> None:
    """Process deposits."""
    print("PROCESSING DEPOSITS...")
    ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        validate_deposit_C1()
        if ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
            post_deposit_C1()
            update_balance_C1()
            ws_counters_C1.ws_tran_count += 1
        ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def validate_deposit_C1() -> None:
    """Validate deposit."""
    global ws_flags_C1
    ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount < 0:
        ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
    if account_record_C1 and account_record_C1.acct_status != 'A':
        ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag = 'N'

def post_deposit_C1() -> None:
    """Post deposit."""
    global account_record_C1, ws_totals_C1
    if account_record_C1:
        account_record_C1.acct_balance += ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount
        account_record_C1.acct_available += ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount
    ws_totals_C1.ws_total_deposits += ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount
    write_transaction_C1()

def update_balance_C1() -> None:
    """Update balance."""
    global account_record_C1
    if account_record_C1:
        account_record_C1.acct_last_trans_date = ws_current_date_data_C1.ws_current_date

def process_withdrawals_C1() -> None:
    """Process withdrawals."""
    print("PROCESSING WITHDRAWALS...")
    ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        validate_withdrawal_C1()
        if ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
            post_withdrawal_C1()
            ws_counters_C1.ws_tran_count += 1
        ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def validate_withdrawal_C1() -> None:
    """Validate withdrawal."""
    global ws_flags_C1
    ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    if account_record_C1 and ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount > account_record_C1.acct_available:
        if account_record_C1 and ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount > (account_record_C1.acct_available + account_record_C1.acct_overdraft_limit):
            ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag = 'N'
        else:
            apply_overdraft_fee_C1()

def apply_overdraft_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Apply overdraft fee."""
    global ws_totals_C1, account_record_C1
    ws_totals_C1.ws_total_fees += ws_fee_schedule_C1.ws_overdraft_fee
    if account_record_C1:
        account_record_C1.acct_balance -= ws_fee_schedule_C1.ws_overdraft_fee

def post_withdrawal_C1() -> None:
    """Post withdrawal."""
    global account_record_C1, ws_totals_C1
    if account_record_C1:
        account_record_C1.acct_balance -= ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount
        account_record_C1.acct_available -= ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount
    ws_totals_C1.ws_total_withdrawals += ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_amount
    write_transaction_C1()

def calculate_interest_C1() -> None:
    """Calculate interest."""
    print("CALCULATING INTEREST...")
    ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        determine_rate_C1()
        compute_interest_C1()
        post_interest_C1()
        ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def determine_rate_C1() -> None:
    """Determine rate."""
    global ws_calculation_fields_C1
    if account_record_C1:
        if account_record_C1.acct_type == 'CH':
            ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_rate = ws_interest_rates_C1.ws_checking_rate
        elif account_record_C1.acct_type == 'SV':
            ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_rate = ws_interest_rates_C1.ws_savings_rate
        elif account_record_C1.acct_type == 'MM':
            ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_rate = ws_interest_rates_C1.ws_mm_rate
        elif account_record_C1.acct_type == 'CD':
            ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_rate = ws_interest_rates_C1.ws_cd_rate_1yr
        else:
            ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_rate = Decimal("0")

def compute_interest_C1() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global ws_calculation_fields_C1
    if account_record_C1:
        ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_interest = account_record_C1.acct_balance * ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_rate / 12

def post_interest_C1() -> None:
    """Post interest."""
    global account_record_C1, ws_totals_C1
    if account_record_C1:
        account_record_C1.acct_balance += ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_interest
    ws_totals_C1.ws_total_interest += ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_interest

def apply_fees_C1() -> None:
    """Apply fees."""
    print("APPLYING MONTHLY FEES...")
    ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        check_minimum_balance_C1()
        if ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag == 'Y':
            waive_fee_C1()
        else:
            charge_fee_C1()
        ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def check_minimum_balance_C1() -> None:
    """Check minimum balance."""
    global ws_flags_C1
    if account_record_C1 and account_record_C1.acct_balance >= account_record_C1.acct_min_balance:
        ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_flags_C1.ws_valid_flag = 'N'

def waive_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Waive fee."""
    pass

def charge_fee_C1() -> None:
    """Charge fee."""
    global account_record_C1, ws_totals_C1
    if account_record_C1:
        account_record_C1.acct_balance -= account_record_C1.acct_monthly_fee
    ws_totals_C1.ws_total_fees += account_record_C1.acct_monthly_fee if account_record_C1 else Decimal("0")

def process_payments_C1() -> None:
    """Process loan payments."""

        if loan_record_C1 and loan_record_C1.loan_status == 'C':
            calculate_payment_C1()
            apply_payment_C1()
            update_loan_C1()
        ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def calculate_payment_C1() -> None:
    """Calculate payment."""
    global ws_calculation_fields_C1
    if loan_record_C1:
        ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_payment = loan_record_C1.loan_payment_amount
        ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_interest = loan_record_C1.loan_current_balance * loan_record_C1.loan_interest_rate / 12
        ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_principal = ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_payment - ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_interest

def apply_payment_C1() -> None:
    """Apply payment."""
    global loan_record_C1, ws_totals_C1
    if loan_record_C1:
        loan_record_C1.loan_current_balance -= ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_principal
    ws_totals_C1.ws_total_payments += ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_payment
    ws_totals_C1.ws_total_interest += ws_calculation_fields_C1.ws_calc_interest

def update_loan_C1() -> None:
    """Update loan."""
    global loan_record_C1
    if loan_record_C1 and loan_record_C1.loan_current_balance <= 0:
        loan_record_C1.loan_status = 'P'

def calculate_amortization_C1() -> None:
    """Calculate amortization schedules."""
    print("CALCULATING AMORTIZATION SCHEDULES...")
    pass

def assess_delinquencies_C1() -> None:
    """Assess delinquent loans."""
    print("ASSESSING DELINQUENT LOANS...")
    ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        check_payment_status_C1()
        if ws_flags_C1.ws_found_flag == 'N':
            mark_delinquent_C1()
            assess_late_fee_C1()
        ws_flags_C1.ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

def check_payment_status_C1() -> None:
    """Check payment status."""
    global ws_flags_C1
    if loan_record_C1 and loan_record_C1.loan_next_payment_date < ws_current_date_data_C1.ws_current_date:
        ws_flags_C1.ws_found_flag = 'N'

def calculate_premiums_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates premiums."""
    print("CALCULATING PREMIUMS...")
    ws_not_eof: bool = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        insurance_master: Any = InsuranceMaster() # Assuming InsuranceMaster is defined elsewhere
        try:
            insurance_master = InsuranceMaster.next() # Assuming InsuranceMaster has a static method next()
        except StopIteration:
            global WS_EOF
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_master)
            apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_master)
            calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_master)


def determine_base_premium_C2(insurance_master: Any) -> None:
    """Determines the base premium."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if insurance_master.ins_type == 'LIFE': # Assuming 'ins_type' field and constants like INS_LIFE
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_LIFE_RATE_PER_1000  # Assuming other global variables
    elif insurance_master.ins_type == 'HEALTH':
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_HEALTH_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif insurance_master.ins_type == 'AUTO':
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_AUTO_BASE_PREMIUM
    elif insurance_master.ins_type == 'HOME':
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = insurance_master.ins_coverage_amount / 1000 * WS_HOME_RATE_PER_1000
    elif insurance_master.ins_type == 'UMBRELLA':
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_UMBRELLA_RATE
    else:
        pass


def apply_risk_factor_C2(insurance_master: Any) -> None:
    """Applies a risk factor."""
    global WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    if insurance_master.ins_claims_count > 2:
        WS_CALC_AMOUNT = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * Decimal('1.25')


def calculate_final_premium_C2(insurance_master: Any) -> None:
    """Calculates the final premium."""
    global WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS
    insurance_master.ins_premium_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT
    WS_TOTAL_PREMIUMS += None  # TODO: was WS_CALC_AMOUNT

def calculate_portfolio_value_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates portfolio values."""
    print("CALCULATING PORTFOLIO VALUES...")
    ws_not_eof: bool = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        investment_master: Any = InvestmentMaster() # Assuming InvestmentMaster is defined elsewhere
        try:
            investment_master = InvestmentMaster.next() # Assuming InvestmentMaster has a static method next()
        except StopIteration:
            global WS_EOF
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            calculate_position_value_C2(investment_master)
            calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_master)
            update_totals_C2(investment_master)


def calculate_position_value_C2(investment_master: Any) -> None:
    """Calculates position value."""
    investment_master.inv_market_value = investment_master.inv_quantity * investment_master.inv_current_price


def calculate_gain_loss_C2(investment_master: Any) -> None:
    """Calculates gain or loss."""
    investment_master.inv_gain_loss = investment_master.inv_market_value - (investment_master.inv_quantity * investment_master.inv_purchase_price)


def update_totals_C2(investment_master: Any) -> None:
    """Updates totals."""
    global WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS
    WS_TOTAL_INVESTMENTS += investment_master.inv_market_value

def calculate_tax_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates tax."""
    global WS_CALC_TAX
    if WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_1_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_2_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE)
    elif WS_CALC_AMOUNT <= WS_BRACKET_3_MAX:
        WS_CALC_TAX = (WS_BRACKET_1_MAX * WS_BRACKET_1_RATE) + ((WS_BRACKET_2_MAX - WS_BRACKET_1_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_2_RATE) + ((WS_CALC_AMOUNT - WS_BRACKET_2_MAX) * WS_BRACKET_3_RATE)
    else:
        WS_CALC_TAX = WS_CALC_AMOUNT * WS_BRACKET_5_RATE

def behavioral_scoring_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates behavioral scores."""
    print("CALCULATING BEHAVIORAL SCORES...")
    ws_not_eof: bool = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        customer_master: Any = CustomerMaster() # Assuming CustomerMaster is defined elsewhere
        try:
            customer_master = CustomerMaster.next() # Assuming CustomerMaster has a static method next()
        except StopIteration:

            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_master)

def calculate_risk_score_C2(customer_master: Any) -> None:
    """Calculates the risk score."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    WS_CALC_RESULT = 0
    if customer_master.cust_credit_score < 600:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 30
    if customer_master.cust_total_loans > customer_master.cust_total_balance:
        WS_CALC_RESULT += 20


def update_customer_profile_C2(customer_master: Any) -> None:
    """Updates the customer profile."""
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > 50:
        customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'H'
    elif WS_CALC_RESULT > 25:
        customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'M'
    else:
        customer_master.cust_risk_rating = 'L'

def dti_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates DTI."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT, WS_NOT_APPROVED
    WS_CALC_RESULT = LOAN_PAYMENT_AMOUNT / (CUST_TOTAL_BALANCE / 12)
    if WS_CALC_RESULT > Decimal('0.43'):
        WS_NOT_APPROVED = True


def ltv_calculation_C2() -> None:
    """Calculates LTV."""
    global LOAN_LTV_RATIO, WS_CALC_FEE
    LOAN_LTV_RATIO = LOAN_CURRENT_BALANCE / LOAN_COLLATERAL_VALUE
    if LOAN_LTV_RATIO > Decimal('0.80'):
        WS_CALC_FEE += WS_LOAN_ORIGINATION_PCT

def portfolio_analysis_C2() -> None:
    """Analyzes portfolios."""
    print("ANALYZING PORTFOLIOS...")
    ws_not_eof: bool = True
    while not WS_EOF:
        investment_master: Any = InvestmentMaster() # Assuming InvestmentMaster is defined elsewhere
        try:
            investment_master = InvestmentMaster.next() # Assuming InvestmentMaster has a static method next()
        except StopIteration:
            global WS_EOF
            WS_EOF = True
        else:
            calculate_returns_C2(investment_master)
            assess_risk_C2()
            benchmark_comparison_C2()


def calculate_returns_C2(investment_master: Any) -> None:
    """Calculates returns."""
    global WS_CALC_RESULT
    if investment_master.inv_purchase_price > 0:
        WS_CALC_RESULT = (investment_master.inv_current_price - investment_master.inv_purchase_price) / investment_master.inv_purchase_price * 100


def assess_risk_C2() -> None:
    """Assesses risk."""
    global WS_TEMP_FLAG
    if INV_STOCKS:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'H'
    elif INV_BONDS:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'L'
    elif INV_MUTUAL_FUND:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'
    else:
        WS_TEMP_FLAG = 'M'

@dataclass
class InsuranceMaster:
    """Represents the Insurance Master Record"""
    ins_type: str
    ins_coverage_amount: Decimal
    ins_claims_count: int
    ins_premium_amount: Decimal
    def next():
        """Dummy iterator"""
        raise StopIteration

@dataclass

    inv_quantity: int
    inv_current_price: Decimal
    inv_purchase_price: Decimal
    inv_market_value: Decimal
    inv_gain_loss: Decimal
    inv_dividend_rate: Decimal
    def next():
        """Dummy iterator"""
        raise StopIteration

    """Represents the Transaction Log Record"""
    tran_amount: Decimal
    def next():
        """Dummy iterator"""
        raise StopIteration

    cust_total_loans: Decimal
    cust_total_balance: Decimal
    cust_risk_rating: str
    def next():
        """Dummy iterator"""

def customer_segmentation_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof
    print("SEGMENTING CUSTOMERS...")
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        try:
            global customer_master
            customer = next(customer_master)
            calculate_clv_C3()
            assign_segment_C3()
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof = True

def calculate_clv_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_result, cust_total_balance, ws_savings_rate, cust_total_loans, ws_personal_rate, cust_total_investments
    ws_calc_result = (cust_total_balance * ws_savings_rate) + (cust_total_loans * ws_personal_rate) + (cust_total_investments * Decimal('0.01'))

def assign_segment_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_result, ws_temp_code
    if ws_calc_result > 10000:
        ws_temp_code = 'PLATINUM'
    elif ws_calc_result > 5000:
        ws_temp_code = 'GOLD'
    elif ws_calc_result > 1000:
        ws_temp_code = 'SILVER'
    else:
        ws_temp_code = 'BRONZE'

    """Brief description."""

        ws_calc_result += 25

def sweep_accounts_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_calc_amount, acct_balance, acct_min_balance, ws_total_investments
    if acct_balance > acct_min_balance:
        ws_calc_amount = acct_balance - acct_min_balance
        acct_balance -= ws_calc_amount
        ws_total_investments += ws_calc_amount

def extract_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_not_eof, ws_eof, ws_process_count
    ws_not_eof = True
    while not ws_eof:
        try:
            global customer_master
            customer = next(customer_master)
            ws_process_count += 1
        except StopIteration:
            ws_eof = True

def transform_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    cleanse_data_C3()
    standardize_data_C3()
    enrich_data_C3()

def cleanse_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global cust_name, cust_last_name
    if cust_name == " ":
        cust_last_name = "UNKNOWN"

def standardize_data_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global cust_state
    cust_state = cust_state.upper()

def completeness_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_error_count, cust_id
    if cust_id == " ":
        ws_error_count += 1

def accuracy_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global ws_error_count, cust_credit_score
    if cust_credit_score < 300 or cust_credit_score > 850:
        ws_error_count += 1

def consistency_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    continue_C3()

def timeliness_check_C3() -> None:
    """Brief description."""
    global cust_last_activity, ws_current_date, ws_error_count
    if cust_last_activity < ws_current_date - 365:
        pass

@dataclass
class Customer:
    cust_id: str = ""
    cust_name: str = ""
    cust_last_name: str = ""
    cust_state: str = ""
    cust_credit_score: int = 0
    cust_last_activity: int = 0
    cust_total_balance: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_total_loans: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
    cust_total_investments: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

loan_delinquent: bool = False
acct_balance: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
acct_min_balance: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')

ws_annual_fee_card: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_total_fees: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_wire_fee_domestic: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_wire_fee_intl: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_calc_amount: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_total_deposits: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_total_withdrawals: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_calc_result: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_savings_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_personal_rate: Decimal = Decimal('0.00')
ws_temp_code: str = ""
ws_not_approved: bool = False
ws_not_eof: bool = False
ws_eof: bool = False
ws_error_count: int = 0
ws_process_count: int = 0
ws_current_date: int = 20240101

customer_master = iter([Customer(cust_id="1", cust_name="John", cust_last_name="Doe", cust_state="CA", cust_credit_score=700, cust_last_activity=20230101, cust_total_balance=1000, cust_total_loans=500, cust_total_investments=2000),
                         Customer(cust_id="2", cust_name="Jane", cust_last_name="Smith", cust_state="NY", cust_credit_score=650, cust_last_activity=20220101, cust_total_balance=2000, cust_total_loans=1000, cust_total_investments=3000)])

@dataclass
class DataRecordC4:
    """Data record."""
    cust_status: Optional[str] = None
    cust_ssn: Optional[str] = None
    cust_credit_score: Optional[int] = None
    cust_risk_rating: Optional[str] = None
    cust_total_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    cust_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    loan_current_balance: Optional[Decimal] = None
    loan_paid_off: Optional[bool] = False

@dataclass
class WorkingStorageC4:
    """Working storage."""
    ws_temp_code: Optional[str] = None
    ws_calc_result: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_deposits: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_loans: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_calc_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_total_fees: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_not_eof: Optional[bool] = False
    ws_eof: Optional[bool] = False
    ws_process_count: Optional[int] = 0
    ws_error_count: Optional[int] = 0
    ws_current_timestamp: Optional[str] = None
    ws_temp_string: Optional[str] = None
    ws_valid: Optional[bool] = False
    ws_atm_fee_foreign: Optional[Decimal] = None
    ws_formatted_count: Optional[str] = None
    ws_cust_count: Optional[int] = 0

@dataclass
class TransactionLogC4:
    """Transaction log."""
    tran_amount: Optional[Decimal] = None

data_record_C4 = DataRecordC4()
working_storage_C4 = WorkingStorageC4()
transaction_log_C4 = TransactionLogC4()

def c100_transaction_monitoring_C4() -> None:
    """Monitoring transactions."""
    print("MONITORING TRANSACTIONS...")
    working_storage_C4.ws_not_eof = True
    while not working_storage_C4.ws_eof:
        # Simulate reading transaction log
        # Assuming transaction_log_C4 is populated here
        c110_rule_based_detection_C4()
        c120_behavior_analysis_C4()
        c130_network_analysis_C4()
        working_storage_C4.ws_eof = True #break infinite loop

def c110_rule_based_detection_C4() -> None:
    """Rule-based detection."""
    if transaction_log_C4.tran_amount and transaction_log_C4.tran_amount >= 10000:
        c111_flag_ctr_C4()
    if transaction_log_C4.tran_amount and 5000 <= transaction_log_C4.tran_amount < 10000:
        c112_check_structuring_C4()

def c111_flag_ctr_C4() -> None:
    """Flag CTR."""
    working_storage_C4.ws_process_count += 1

def c112_check_structuring_C4() -> None:
    """Check structuring."""
    working_storage_C4.ws_error_count += 1

def d110_classification_C4() -> None:
    """Classification."""
    if data_record_C4.cust_credit_score and data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 750:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'A'
    elif data_record_C4.cust_credit_score and data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 650:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'B'
    elif data_record_C4.cust_credit_score and data_record_C4.cust_credit_score > 550:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'C'
    else:
        data_record_C4.cust_risk_rating = 'D'

def d120_regression_C4() -> None:
    """Regression."""
    data_record = data_record_C4
    working_storage_C4.ws_calc_result = (
        (data_record.cust_credit_score or 0) * 10 + 0  # TODO
        (data_record.cust_total_balance or 0) / 1000 - 0  # TODO
        (data_record.cust_total_loans or 0) / 2000
    )

def main_logic_C5() -> None:
    """Main loop."""
    global WS_NOT_EOF_C5
    WS_NOT_EOF_C5 = True
    while not WS_EOF_C5:
        read_customer_master_next_C5()

def read_customer_master_next_C5() -> None:
    """Read customer master."""
    global WS_EOF_C5, WS_CUST_COUNT_C5
    # Simulate READ customer_master NEXT
    # In a real system, this would involve file I/O
    customer_record = None # Replace with actual file read

    if customer_record is None:
        WS_EOF_C5 = True
    else:
        update_profile_I110_C5()
        enrich_profile_I120_C5()
        WS_CUST_COUNT_C5 += 1

def initialization_1000_C5() -> None:
    """Initialization."""
    global WS_WORK_AREAS_C5, WS_COUNTERS_C5, WS_TOTALS_C5, WS_CURRENT_DATETIME_C5, RPT_YEAR_C5, RPT_MONTH_C5, RPT_DAY_C5
    WS_WORK_AREAS_C5 = "" # INITIALIZE ws_work_areas

    WS_TOTALS_C5 = Decimal("0.00") # INITIALIZE ws_totals

    # Simulate current_date
    WS_CURRENT_DATETIME_C5 = "20240101"  # Replace with actual date/time
    RPT_YEAR_C5  = WS_CURRENT_DATETIME_C5[:4]
    RPT_MONTH_C5 = WS_CURRENT_DATETIME_C5[4:6]
    RPT_DAY_C5   = WS_CURRENT_DATETIME_C5[6:8]
    open_files_1100_C5()

def load_reference_data_1400_C5() -> None:
    """Load reference data."""
    global WS_TBL_IDX_C5, WS_EOF_FLAG_C5, WS_REF_RECORD_C5, RT_CODE_C5, RT_RATE_C5
    WS_TBL_IDX_C5 = 1
    WS_EOF_FLAG_C5 = 'N'

    while WS_EOF_FLAG_C5 != 'Y' and WS_TBL_IDX_C5 <= 100:
        # Simulate READ reference_file
        reference_record = None  # Replace with actual file read
        if reference_record is None:
            WS_EOF_FLAG_C5 = 'Y'
        else:
            WS_REF_RECORD_C5 = ReferenceRecordC5()
            # Assume WS_REF_RECORD contains WS_REF_CODE and WS_REF_RATE
            WS_REF_RECORD_C5 = reference_record
            RT_CODE_C5[WS_TBL_IDX_C5 - 1] = "REFCODE"  # Replace with WS_REF_RECORD_C5.code
            RT_RATE_C5[WS_TBL_IDX_C5 - 1] = Decimal("1.23")  # Replace with WS_REF_RECORD_C5.rate
            WS_TBL_IDX_C5 += 1

    WS_EOF_FLAG_C5 = 'N'

def process_transactions_2000_C5() -> None:
    """Process transactions."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG_C5, WS_TRANS_COUNT_C5, WS_TRANSACTION_REC_C5
    # Simulate READ transaction_file
    transaction_record = None # Replace with actual file read

    if transaction_record is None:
        WS_EOF_FLAG_C5 = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_TRANS_COUNT_C5 += 1
        WS_TRANSACTION_REC_C5 = TransactionRecordC5()
        validate_transaction_2100_C5()
        if WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 == 'Y':
            process_by_type_2200_C5()
        else:
            handle_error_2900_C5()

def validate_transaction_2100_C5() -> None:
    """Validate transaction."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, TXN_TYPE_C5
    WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'Y'
    TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5 = "12345" # Simulate
    TXN_AMOUNT_C5 = Decimal("100.00") # Simulate
    TXN_TYPE_C5 = "D" # Simulate

    if TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5 == "" or TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5 == "": # SPACES or low_values
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID ACCOUNT ID'
        return # EXIT PARAGRAPH

    try:
        float(TXN_AMOUNT_C5)
    except ValueError:
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
        return # EXIT PARAGRAPH

    if TXN_TYPE_C5 not in ('D', 'W', 'T', 'I'):
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INVALID TRANSACTION TYPE'

    validate_account_exists_2150_C5()
    validate_business_rules_2160_C5()

def validate_account_exists_2150_C5() -> None:
    """Validate account exists."""
    global WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5, WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5, TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5
    WS_SEARCH_KEY_C5  = None  # TODO: was TXN_ACCOUNT_ID_C5
    search_account_5000_C5()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG_C5 == 'N':
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'ACCOUNT NOT FOUND'

def validate_business_rules_2160_C5() -> None:
    """Validate business rules."""
    global WS_VALID_FLAG_C5, WS_ERROR_MSG_C5, TXN_TYPE_C5, TXN_AMOUNT_C5, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5
    if TXN_TYPE_C5 == 'W':
        if TXN_AMOUNT_C5 > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE_C5:
            WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
            WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'

    if TXN_AMOUNT_C5 > Decimal("1000000.00"):
        WS_VALID_FLAG_C5 = 'N'
        WS_ERROR_MSG_C5 = 'AMOUNT EXCEEDS LIMIT'

        handle_error_2900_C5()

def calculate_pmi_C7(ws_ltv_ratio: float, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> Decimal:
    """Calculates the PMI amount."""
    if ws_ltv_ratio > 95:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0125') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 90:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0100') / 12
    elif ws_ltv_ratio > 85:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0075') / 12
    else:
        ws_pmi_amount = ws_loan_amount * Decimal('0.0050') / 12
    return ws_pmi_amount

def evaluate_history_C7(ws_late_90_days: int, ws_late_60_days: int, ws_late_30_days: int, ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str, str, str]:
    """Evaluates delinquency history and adjusts risk score."""
    ws_factor_1 = ""
    ws_factor_2 = ""
    ws_factor_3 = ""
    if ws_late_90_days > 0:
        ws_risk_score -= 50
        ws_factor_1 = 'SEVERE DELINQUENCY HISTORY'
    if ws_late_60_days > 2:
        ws_risk_score -= 30
        ws_factor_2 = '60+ DAY DELINQUENCIES'
    if ws_late_30_days > 5:
        ws_risk_score -= 20
        ws_factor_3 = 'MULTIPLE 30-DAY LATES'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_factor_1, ws_factor_2, ws_factor_3

def calculate_final_risk_C7(ws_risk_score: int) -> tuple[int, str]:
    """Calculates the final risk score and category."""
    ws_risk_score = ws_risk_score / 4
    ws_risk_category = ""
    if ws_risk_score >= 80:
        ws_risk_category = 'LOW RISK'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 60:
        ws_risk_category = 'MODERATE'
    elif ws_risk_score >= 40:
        ws_risk_category = 'ELEVATED'
    else:
        ws_risk_category = 'HIGH RISK'
    return ws_risk_score, ws_risk_category

def determine_approval_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_risk_category: str, ws_dti_ratio: float, ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_base_rate: Decimal) -> tuple[str, str, Decimal]:
    """Determines loan approval status and conditions."""
    ws_approval_status = ""
    ws_conditions = ""
    ws_approved_amount = Decimal("0.00")
    ws_approved_rate = Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_credit_tier == 'F':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'CREDIT SCORE TOO LOW'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_risk_category == 'HIGH RISK':
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'RISK ASSESSMENT FAILED'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    if ws_dti_ratio > 50:
        ws_approval_status = 'D'
        ws_conditions = 'DTI RATIO TOO HIGH'
        return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate
    ws_approval_status = 'A'
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
    return ws_approval_status, ws_conditions, ws_approved_rate

def calculate_approved_terms_C7(ws_credit_tier: str, ws_base_rate: Decimal, ws_risk_category: str, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates approved loan terms."""
    ws_approved_amount = ws_loan_amount
    if ws_credit_tier == 'A':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.00')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'B':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('0.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'C':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('1.50')
    elif ws_credit_tier == 'D':
        ws_approved_rate = ws_base_rate + Decimal('3.00')
    else:
        ws_approved_rate = Decimal("0.00")
    if ws_risk_category == 'ELEVATED':
        ws_approved_rate += Decimal('0.50')
    return ws_approved_amount, ws_approved_rate

def generate_loan_terms_C7(ws_approved_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_loan_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Generates loan terms."""
    ws_loan_interest_rate = ws_approved_rate
    ws_monthly_rate = ws_loan_interest_rate / 1200
    ws_compound_factor = (1 + ws_monthly_rate) ** ws_loan_term_months
    ws_loan_monthly_pmt = ws_loan_amount * ws_monthly_rate * ws_compound_factor / (ws_compound_factor - 1)
    ws_loan_principal_bal = ws_loan_amount
    return ws_loan_interest_rate, ws_monthly_rate, ws_compound_factor, ws_loan_monthly_pmt

def create_amortization_C7(ws_loan_amount: Decimal, ws_loan_term_months: int, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal) -> tuple[List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[Decimal], List[int], List[Decimal], List[Decimal]]:
    """Creates an amortization schedule."""
    ws_running_balance = ws_loan_amount
    import datetime
    today = datetime.date.today()
    ws_payment_year = today.year
    ws_payment_month = today.month
    amort_interest: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_principal: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_balance: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_payment_num: List[int] = []
    amort_payment_amt: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_escrow: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_total_pmt: List[Decimal] = []
    amort_payment_date: List[int] = []

    for ws_amort_idx in range(1, ws_loan_term_months + 1):
        amort_interest.append(ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate)
        amort_principal.append(ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest[ws_amort_idx-1])
        ws_running_balance -= amort_principal[ws_amort_idx-1]
        amort_balance.append(ws_running_balance)
        amort_payment_num.append(ws_amort_idx)
        amort_payment_amt.append(ws_loan_monthly_pmt)

        if loan_mortgage:
            escrow = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
            amort_escrow.append(escrow)
            total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + escrow + ws_pmi_amount
            amort_total_pmt.append(total_pmt)
        else:
            amort_total_pmt.append(ws_loan_monthly_pmt)

        ws_payment_month += 1
        if ws_payment_month > 12:
            ws_payment_month = 1
            ws_payment_year += 1
        payment_date = ws_payment_year * 10000 + ws_payment_month * 100 + 1
        amort_payment_date.append(payment_date)

    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_balance, amort_payment_num, amort_payment_amt, amort_total_pmt

def calculate_payment_split_C7(ws_running_balance: Decimal, ws_monthly_rate: Decimal, ws_loan_monthly_pmt: Decimal, ws_amort_idx: int, ws_property_tax: Decimal, ws_insurance_premium: Decimal, ws_pmi_amount: Decimal, loan_mortgage: bool) -> tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Decimal]:
    """Calculates the payment split between interest and principal."""
    amort_interest = ws_running_balance * ws_monthly_rate
    amort_principal = ws_loan_monthly_pmt - amort_interest
    ws_running_balance -= amort_principal
    amort_balance = ws_running_balance
    amort_payment_num = ws_amort_idx
    amort_payment_amt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    if loan_mortgage:
        amort_escrow = (ws_property_tax + ws_insurance_premium) / 12
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt + amort_escrow + ws_pmi_amount
    else:
        amort_total_pmt = ws_loan_monthly_pmt
    return amort_interest, amort_principal, amort_total_pmt

def advance_payment_date_C7(ws_payment_month: int, ws_payment_year: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Advances the payment date by one month."""
    ws_payment_month += 1
    if ws_payment_month > 12:
        ws_payment_month = 1
        ws_payment_year += 1
    return ws_payment_month, ws_payment_year

def calc_auto_premium_C8(ws_driver_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_violations_3yr: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates auto premium."""
    if 6 <= ws_driver_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium += 100
    else:
        ws_base_premium += 50
    if ws_driver_age < 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_accidents_3yr > 0:
        ws_accident_surcharge = ws_accidents_3yr * 200
        ws_base_premium += ws_accident_surcharge
    if ws_violations_3yr > 0:
        ws_violation_surcharge = ws_violations_3yr * 100
        ws_base_premium += ws_violation_surcharge
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12
    pass

def calc_home_premium_C8(ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_home_age: int, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_flood_zone: str, ws_security_system: str, ws_deductible: int, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates home premium."""
    ws_base_premium = ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.003')
    if 0 <= ws_home_age <= 10:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    elif 11 <= ws_home_age <= 25:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 26 <= ws_home_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.2')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_flood_zone == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    if ws_security_system == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    ws_deductible_credit = ws_deductible / 1000 * 50
    ws_base_premium -= ws_deductible_credit
    if ws_base_premium < 200:
        ws_base_premium = Decimal('200')
    ws_annual_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_annual_premium / 12
    pass

def calc_health_premium_C8(ws_insured_age: int, ws_plan_type: str, ws_family_plan: str, ws_base_premium: Decimal, ws_monthly_premium: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates health premium."""
    ws_base_premium = Decimal('300')
    if 0 <= ws_insured_age <= 18:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.5')
    elif 19 <= ws_insured_age <= 30:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif 31 <= ws_insured_age <= 40:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif 41 <= ws_insured_age <= 50:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    elif 51 <= ws_insured_age <= 60:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.0')
    else:
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.8')
    if ws_plan_type == 'BRONZE':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('0.8')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'SILVER':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.0')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'GOLD':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.3')
    elif ws_plan_type == 'PLATINUM':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('1.6')
    if ws_family_plan == 'Y':
        ws_base_premium *= Decimal('2.5')
    ws_monthly_premium = ws_base_premium
    ws_annual_premium = ws_monthly_premium * 12
    pass

def underwriting_C8(policy_life: bool, policy_auto: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_doc_missing: str, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str, ws_uw_status: str, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Performs underwriting."""
    evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life, policy_auto, ws_bmi, ws_smoker_flag, ws_hazardous_occupation, ws_driver_age, ws_accidents_3yr, ws_risk_points)
    check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions, ws_recent_hospitalization, ws_prescription_count, ws_risk_points)
    verify_information_C8(ws_recent_claims, ws_address_mismatch, ws_doc_missing, ws_risk_points, ws_fraud_flag, ws_uw_status)
    determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points, ws_uw_decision, ws_annual_premium)
    pass

def evaluate_risk_factors_C8(policy_life: bool, policy_auto: bool, ws_bmi: int, ws_smoker_flag: str, ws_hazardous_occupation: str, ws_driver_age: int, ws_accidents_3yr: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Evaluates risk factors."""
    ws_risk_points = 0
    if policy_life:
        if ws_bmi > 30:
            ws_risk_points += 10
        if ws_smoker_flag == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 25
        if ws_hazardous_occupation == 'Y':
            ws_risk_points += 15
    if policy_auto:
        if ws_driver_age < 21:
            ws_risk_points += 20
        if ws_accidents_3yr > 1:
            ws_risk_points += 15
    pass

def check_medical_history_C8(ws_chronic_conditions: int, ws_recent_hospitalization: str, ws_prescription_count: int, ws_risk_points: int) -> None:
    """Checks medical history."""
    if ws_chronic_conditions > 0:
        ws_condition_points = ws_chronic_conditions * 5
        ws_risk_points += ws_condition_points
    if ws_recent_hospitalization == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    if ws_prescription_count > 5:
        ws_risk_points += 5
    pass

def verify_information_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_doc_missing: str, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Verifies information."""
    check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims, ws_address_mismatch, ws_risk_points, ws_fraud_flag)
    validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing, ws_uw_status)
    pass

def check_fraud_indicators_C8(ws_recent_claims: int, ws_address_mismatch: str, ws_risk_points: int, ws_fraud_flag: str) -> None:
    """Checks fraud indicators."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 3:
        ws_risk_points += 20
        ws_fraud_flag = 'Y'
    if ws_address_mismatch == 'Y':
        ws_risk_points += 10
    pass

def validate_documents_C8(ws_doc_missing: str, ws_uw_status: str) -> None:
    """Validates documents."""
    if ws_doc_missing == 'Y':
        ws_uw_status = 'PENDING'
    else:
        ws_uw_status = 'COMPLETE'
    pass

def determine_decision_C8(ws_risk_points: int, ws_uw_decision: str, ws_annual_premium: Decimal) -> None:
    """Determines decision."""
    if ws_risk_points > 50:
        ws_uw_decision = 'DECLINE'
    elif ws_risk_points > 30:
        ws_uw_decision = 'SUBSTANDARD'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('1.5')
    elif ws_risk_points > 15:
        ws_uw_decision = 'STANDARD'
    else:
        ws_uw_decision = 'PREFERRED'
        ws_annual_premium *= Decimal('0.9')
    pass

def issue_policy_C8(ws_uw_decision: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_effective_date: str, ws_expiration_date: str, ws_policy_number: str, ws_date_part: str, ws_type_part: str, ws_random_part: Decimal, ws_policy_record: str, policy_rec_number: str, policy_rec_type: str, policy_rec_coverage: Decimal, policy_rec_premium: Decimal, policy_rec_eff_date: str, policy_rec_exp_date: str, policy_record: str, ws_benef_idx: int, benef_name: List[str], benef_relation: List[str], benef_pct: List[Decimal], ws_beneficiary_rec: str, benef_rec_policy: str, benef_rec_name: str, benef_rec_relation: str, benef_rec_pct: Decimal, beneficiary_record: str, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """Issues policy."""
    if ws_uw_decision != 'DECLINE':
        generate_policy_number_C8(ws_policy_type, ws_policy_number, ws_date_part, ws_type_part, ws_random_part)
        create_policy_record_C8(ws_policy_number, ws_policy_type, ws_coverage_amount, ws_annual_premium, ws_effective_date, ws_expiration_date, ws_policy_record, policy_rec_number, policy_rec_type, policy_rec_coverage, policy_rec_premium, policy_rec_eff_date, policy_rec_exp_date, policy_record)
        set_beneficiaries_C8(ws_policy_number, benef_name, benef_relation, benef_pct, ws_benef_idx, ws_beneficiary_rec, benef_rec_policy, benef_rec_name, benef_rec_relation, benef_rec_pct, beneficiary_record)
        send_policy_docs_C8(ws_policy_number, ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
    else:
        send_decline_letter_C8(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
    pass

def generate_policy_number_C8(ws_policy_type: str, ws_policy_number: str, ws_date_part: str, ws_type_part: str, ws_random_part: Decimal) -> None:
    """Generates policy number."""
    ws_date_part = "CURRENT_DATE"
    ws_type_part = ws_policy_type
    ws_random_part = Decimal(str(random_C8() * 99999))
    ws_policy_number = f"{ws_type_part}{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"
    pass


def random_C8() -> float:
    """Returns a random float."""
    return random.random()

def create_policy_record_C8(ws_policy_number: str, ws_policy_type: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_annual_premium: Decimal, ws_effective_date: str, ws_expiration_date: str, ws_policy_record: str, policy_rec_number: str, policy_rec_type: str, policy_rec_coverage: Decimal, policy_rec_premium: Decimal, policy_rec_eff_date: str, policy_rec_exp_date: str, policy_record: str) -> None:
    """Creates policy record."""
    ws_policy_record = ""
    policy_rec_number = ws_policy_number
    policy_rec_type = ws_policy_type
    policy_rec_coverage = ws_coverage_amount
    policy_rec_premium = ws_annual_premium
    policy_rec_eff_date = ws_effective_date
    policy_rec_exp_date = ws_expiration_date
    policy_record = ws_policy_record + "A"
    pass

def set_beneficiaries_C8(ws_policy_number: str, benef_name: List[str], benef_relation: List[str], benef_pct: List[Decimal], ws_benef_idx: int, ws_beneficiary_rec: str, benef_rec_policy: str, benef_rec_name: str, benef_rec_relation: str, benef_rec_pct: Decimal, beneficiary_record: str) -> None:
    """Sets beneficiaries."""
    ws_benef_idx = 1
    while ws_benef_idx <= 5:
        if benef_name[ws_benef_idx-1] != "SPACES":
            ws_beneficiary_rec = ""
            benef_rec_policy = ws_policy_number
            benef_rec_name = benef_name[ws_benef_idx-1]
            benef_rec_relation = benef_relation[ws_benef_idx-1]
            benef_rec_pct = benef_pct[ws_benef_idx-1]
            beneficiary_record = ws_beneficiary_rec
        ws_benef_idx += 1
    pass

def send_policy_docs_C8(ws_policy_number: str, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """Sends policy documents."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_issue'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Your policy {ws_policy_number} has been issued'
    send_notification_C8(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
    pass

def send_decline_letter_C8(ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """Sends decline letter."""
    ws_notif_type = 'policy_decline'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Regarding your insurance application'
    send_notification_C8(ws_notif_type, ws_notif_channel, ws_notif_subject)
    pass

def claims_handling_C8(ws_claim_date: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_number: str, ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_deny_reason: str, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str, ws_approved_amount: Decimal, ws_payment_record: str, pay_rec_claim: str, pay_rec_amount: Decimal, pay_rec_date: str, pay_rec_method: str, payment_record: str, ws_claim_close_date: str, claim_record: str) -> None:
    """Handles claims."""
    receive_claim_C8(ws_claim_date, ws_claim_status, ws_claim_number)
    validate_claim_C8(ws_policy_status, ws_claim_type, ws_covered_perils, ws_claim_amount, ws_deductible, ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason)
    investigate_claim_C8(ws_claim_amount, ws_coverage_amount, ws_claim_status, ws_adjuster_id, ws_notes, ws_recent_claims, ws_fraud_review)
    adjudicate_claim_C8(ws_claim_status, ws_claim_amount, ws_deductible, ws_coverage_amount, ws_approved_amount)
    process_payment_C8(ws_claim_status, ws_claim_number, ws_approved_amount, ws_claim_close_date, ws_payment_record, pay_rec_claim, pay_rec_amount, pay_rec_date, pay_rec_method, payment_record, claim_record)
    pass

def receive_claim_C8(ws_claim_date: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_number: str) -> None:
    """Receives claim."""
    ws_claim_date = "CURRENT_DATE"
    generate_claim_number_C8(ws_claim_number, ws_claim_date)
    ws_claim_status = 'RECEIVED'
    pass

def generate_claim_number_C8(ws_claim_number: str, ws_date_part: str) -> None:
    """Generates claim number."""
    ws_date_part = "CURRENT_DATE"
    ws_random_part = Decimal(str(random_C8() * 99999))
    ws_claim_number = f"CLM{ws_date_part}{ws_random_part}"
    pass

def validate_claim_C8(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Validates claim."""
    check_policy_status_C8(ws_policy_status, ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason)
    check_coverage_C8(ws_claim_type, ws_covered_perils, ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason)
    check_deductible_C8(ws_claim_amount, ws_deductible, ws_claim_status, ws_claim_deny_reason)
    pass

def check_policy_status_C8(ws_policy_status: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks policy status."""
    if ws_policy_status != 'A':
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'POLICY NOT ACTIVE'
    pass

def check_coverage_C8(ws_claim_type: str, ws_covered_perils: str, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks coverage."""
    if ws_claim_type != ws_covered_perils:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'NOT COVERED PERIL'
    pass

def check_deductible_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_deny_reason: str) -> None:
    """Checks deductible."""
    if ws_claim_amount <= ws_deductible:
        ws_claim_status = 'DENIED'
        ws_claim_deny_reason = 'BELOW DEDUCTIBLE'
    pass

def investigate_claim_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_status: str, ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Investigates claim."""
    if ws_claim_amount > 10000:
        ws_claim_status = 'INVESTIGATION'
        assign_adjuster_C8(ws_adjuster_id, ws_notes)
    fraud_check_C8(ws_claim_amount, ws_coverage_amount, ws_recent_claims, ws_fraud_review)
    pass

def assign_adjuster_C8(ws_adjuster_id: str, ws_notes: str) -> None:
    """Assigns adjuster."""
    ws_adjuster_id = 'ADJ001'
    ws_notes = 'Assigned for investigation'
    pass

def fraud_check_C8(ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_recent_claims: int, ws_fraud_review: str) -> None:
    """Checks for fraud."""
    if ws_recent_claims > 2:
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    if ws_claim_amount > ws_coverage_amount * Decimal('0.8'):
        ws_fraud_review = 'Y'
    pass

def adjudicate_claim_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_amount: Decimal, ws_deductible: Decimal, ws_coverage_amount: Decimal, ws_approved_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Adjudicates claim."""
    if ws_claim_status != 'DENIED':
        ws_approved_amount = ws_claim_amount - ws_deductible
        if ws_approved_amount > ws_coverage_amount:
            ws_approved_amount = ws_coverage_amount
        ws_claim_status = 'APPROVED'
    pass

def process_payment_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_number: str, ws_approved_amount: Decimal, ws_claim_close_date: str, ws_payment_record: str, pay_rec_claim: str, pay_rec_amount: Decimal, pay_rec_date: str, pay_rec_method: str, payment_record: str, claim_record: str) -> None:
    """Processes payment."""
    if ws_claim_status == 'APPROVED':
        issue_payment_C8(ws_claim_number, ws_approved_amount, ws_payment_record, pay_rec_claim, pay_rec_amount, pay_rec_date, pay_rec_method, payment_record)
        update_claim_record_C8(ws_claim_status, ws_claim_close_date, claim_record)
    pass

def issue_payment_C8(ws_claim_number: str, ws_approved_amount: Decimal, ws_payment_record: str, pay_rec_claim: str, pay_rec_amount: Decimal, pay_rec_date: str, pay_rec_method: str, payment_record: str) -> None:
    """Issues payment."""
    ws_payment_record = ""
    pay_rec_claim = ws_claim_number
    pay_rec_amount = ws_approved_amount
    pay_rec_date = "CURRENT_DATE"
    pay_rec_method = 'CHECK'
    payment_record = ws_payment_record
    pass

def update_claim_record_C8(ws_claim_status: str, ws_claim_close_date: str, claim_record: str) -> None:
    """Updates claim record."""
    ws_claim_status = 'PAID'
    ws_claim_close_date = "CURRENT_DATE"
    claim_record = ""
    pass

def payroll_processing_C8(ws_employee_id: str, ws_employee_rec: str, emp_search_key: str, emp_id: str, employee_file: str, ws_error_msg: str, ws_pay_type: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_hours_worked: Decimal, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: Decimal, ws_base_pay: Decimal, ws_base_salary: Decimal, ws_commission_pay: Decimal, ws_sales_amount: Decimal, ws_commission_rate: Decimal, ws_annualized_gross: Decimal, ws_exemptions: int, ws_allowance_amount: Decimal, ws_taxable_income: Decimal, ws_annual_tax: Decimal, status_single: bool, status_married_joint: bool, ws_federal_tax: Decimal, ws_state_code: str, ws_state_tax: Decimal, ws_local_tax_rate: Decimal, ws_local_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_gross: Decimal, ws_remaining_cap: Decimal, ws_fica_ss: Decimal, ws_fica_medicare: Decimal, ws_additional_medicare: Decimal, ws_401k_pct: Decimal, ws_401k_contrib: Decimal, ws_ytd_401k: Decimal, ws_health_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_health_ins: Decimal, ws_dental_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_dental_ins: Decimal, ws_vision_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_vision_ins: Decimal, ws_hsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_hsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_fsa_deduct: Decimal, ws_fsa_contrib: Decimal, ws_life_ins_deduct: Decimal, ws_life_ins: Decimal, ws_disability_deduct: Decimal, ws_disability_ins: Decimal, ws_union_dues_amt: Decimal, ws_union_dues: Decimal, ws_garnishmen_amt: Decimal, ws_garnishmen: Decimal, ws_other_deduct: Decimal, ws_total_deductions: Decimal, ws_net_pay: Decimal, ws_ytd_fed_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_state_tax: Decimal, ws_ytd_fica: Decimal, ws_ytd_net: Decimal, ws_paystub_record: str, stub_emp_id: str, stub_pay_period: str, stub_gross: Decimal, stub_fed_tax: Decimal, stub_state_tax: Decimal, stub_ss: Decimal, stub_medicare: Decimal, stub_net: Decimal, stub_ytd_gross: Decimal, stub_ytd_net: Decimal, paystub_record: str, ws_dd_enabled: str, ws_routing_number: str, ws_account_number: str, ws_dd_valid: str, ws_pay_date: str, ws_ach_record: str, ach_routing: str, ach_account: str, ach_amount: Decimal, ach_date: str, ach_desc: str, ach_record: str) -> None:
    """Processes payroll."""
    load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id, ws_employee_rec, emp_search_key, emp_id, employee_file, ws_error_msg)
    calculate_gross_pay_C8(ws_pay_type, ws_gross_pay, ws_annual_salary, ws_pay_periods, ws_hours_worked, ws_hourly_rate, ws_regular_pay, ws_overtime_pay, ws_ot_hours, ws_base_pay, ws_base_salary, ws_commission_pay, ws_sales_amount, ws_commission_rate)
    calculate_taxes_C8(ws_gross_pay, ws_annualized_gross, ws_exemptions, ws_allowance_amount, ws_taxable_income, ws_annual_tax, status_single, status_married_joint, ws_federal_tax, ws_state_code, ws_state_tax, ws_local_tax_rate, ws_local_tax, ws_ytd_gross, ws_remaining_cap, ws_fica_ss, ws_fica_medicare, ws_additional_medicare)
    calculate_deductions_C8(ws_gross_pay, ws_401k_pct, ws_401k_contrib, ws_ytd_401k, ws_health_ins_deduct, ws_health_ins, ws_dental_ins_deduct, ws_dental_ins, ws_vision_ins_deduct, ws_vision_ins, ws_hsa_deduct, ws_hsa_contrib, ws_fsa_deduct, ws_fsa_contrib, ws_life_ins_deduct, ws_life_ins, ws_disability_deduct, ws_disability_ins, ws_union_dues_amt, ws_union_dues, ws_garnishmen_amt, ws_garnishmen, ws_other_deduct)
    calculate_net_pay_C8(ws_gross_pay, ws_federal_tax, ws_state_tax, ws_local_tax, ws_fica_ss, ws_fica_medicare, ws_health_ins, ws_dental_ins, ws_vision_ins, ws_401k_contrib, ws_hsa_contrib, ws_fsa_contrib, ws_life_ins, ws_disability_ins, ws_union_dues, ws_garnishmen, ws_other_deduct, ws_total_deductions, ws_net_pay, ws_ytd_gross, ws_ytd_fed_tax, ws_ytd_state_tax, ws_ytd_fica, ws_ytd_net, ws_ytd_401k)
    generate_paystubs_C8(ws_employee_id, ws_pay_period, ws_gross_pay, ws_federal_tax, ws_state_tax, ws_fica_ss, ws_fica_medicare, ws_net_pay, ws_ytd_gross, ws_ytd_net, ws_paystub_record, stub_emp_id, stub_pay_period, stub_gross, stub_fed_tax, stub_state_tax, stub_ss, stub_medicare, stub_net, stub_ytd_gross, stub_ytd_net, paystub_record)
    process_direct_deposit_C8(ws_dd_enabled, ws_routing_number, ws_account_number, ws_dd_valid, ws_net_pay, ws_pay_date, ws_ach_record, ach_routing, ach_account, ach_amount, ach_date, ach_desc, ach_record)
    pass

def load_employee_data_C8(ws_employee_id: str, ws_employee_rec: str, emp_search_key: str, emp_id: str, employee_file: str, ws_error_msg: str) -> None:
    """Loads employee data."""
    emp_search_key = ws_employee_id
    ws_employee_rec = ""
    if emp_search_key != emp_id:
        ws_error_msg = 'EMPLOYEE NOT FOUND'
        handle_error_C8(ws_error_msg)
    pass

def handle_error_C8(ws_error_msg: str) -> None:
    """Handles error."""
    pass

def calculate_gross_pay_C8(ws_pay_type: str, ws_gross_pay: Decimal, ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_hours_worked: Decimal, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: Decimal, ws_base_pay: Decimal, ws_base_salary: Decimal, ws_commission_pay: Decimal, ws_sales_amount: Decimal, ws_commission_rate: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates gross pay."""
    if ws_pay_type == 'SALARY':
        calc_salary_pay_C8(ws_annual_salary, ws_pay_periods, ws_gross_pay)
    elif ws_pay_type == 'HOURLY':
        calc_hourly_pay_C8(ws_hours_worked, ws_hourly_rate, ws_regular_pay, ws_overtime_pay, ws_ot_hours, ws_gross_pay)
    elif ws_pay_type == 'COMMISSION':
        calc_commission_pay_C8(ws_base_salary, ws_pay_periods, ws_sales_amount, ws_commission_rate, ws_base_pay, ws_commission_pay, ws_gross_pay)
    pass

def calc_salary_pay_C8(ws_annual_salary: Decimal, ws_pay_periods: int, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates salary pay."""
    ws_gross_pay = ws_annual_salary / ws_pay_periods
    pass

def calc_hourly_pay_C8(ws_hours_worked: Decimal, ws_hourly_rate: Decimal, ws_regular_pay: Decimal, ws_overtime_pay: Decimal, ws_ot_hours: Decimal, ws_gross_pay: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculates hourly pay."""
    if ws_hours_worked <= 40:
        ws_regular_pay = ws_hours_worked * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_overtime_pay = Decimal('0')
    else:
        ws_regular_pay = 40 * ws_hourly_rate
        ws_ot_hours = ws_hours_worked - 40
        ws_overtime_pay = ws_ot_hours * ws_hourly_rate * Decimal('1.5')
    ws_gross_pay = ws_regular_pay + ws_overtime_pay
    pass
