def calculate_next_run_date_C10(ws_last_run_date: Any, ws_next_run_date: Any, ws_frequency: str) -> None:
    """Calculates the next run date based on frequency."""
    if ws_frequency == 'DAILY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 1
    elif ws_frequency == 'WEEKLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 7
    elif ws_frequency == 'MONTHLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 30
    elif ws_frequency == 'QUARTERLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 90
    elif ws_frequency == 'YEARLY':
        ws_next_run_date = int(ws_last_run_date) + 365
    else:
        pass

def data_analytics_C10() -> None:
    """Performs data analytics and reporting procedures."""
    collect_metrics_C10()
    aggregate_data_C10()
    calculate_kpi_C10()
    generate_dashboard_C10()
    export_data_C10()

def collect_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects various metrics."""
    collect_transaction_metrics_C10()
    collect_customer_metrics_C10()
    collect_performance_metrics_C10()

def collect_transaction_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects transaction-related metrics."""
    global ws_total_trans_amount, ws_total_trans_count, ws_avg_trans_amount, ws_eof_flag
    ws_total_trans_amount = 0
    ws_total_trans_count = 0
    ws_avg_trans_amount = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_trans_rec = transaction_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_trans_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                ws_total_trans_count += 1
                ws_total_trans_amount += Decimal(transaction_amount(ws_trans_rec))
        except FileNotFoundError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_avg_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount / ws_total_trans_count

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_customer_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects customer-related metrics."""
    global ws_active_customers, ws_new_customers, ws_churned_customers, ws_eof_flag
    ws_active_customers = 0
    ws_new_customers = 0
    ws_churned_customers = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_cust_rec = customer_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_cust_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                if customer_status(ws_cust_rec) == 'A':
                    ws_active_customers += 1
                if customer_open_date(ws_cust_rec) >= ws_period_start:
                    ws_new_customers += 1
                if customer_close_date(ws_cust_rec) >= ws_period_start:
                    ws_churned_customers += 1
        except FileNotFoundError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def collect_performance_metrics_C10() -> None:
    """Collects performance-related metrics."""
    global ws_response_time_total, ws_response_count, ws_avg_response_time, ws_eof_flag
    ws_response_time_total = 0
    ws_response_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_perf_rec = perf_log_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_perf_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                ws_response_time_total += Decimal(perf_response_time(ws_perf_rec))
                ws_response_count += 1
        except FileNotFoundError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    if ws_response_count > 0:
        ws_avg_response_time = ws_response_time_total / ws_response_count

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def aggregate_data_C10() -> None:
    """Aggregates data."""
    daily_aggregation_C10()
    weekly_aggregation_C10()
    monthly_aggregation_C10()

def daily_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs daily data aggregation."""
    global ws_daily_summary
    ws_daily_summary = DailySummary()
    ws_daily_summary.daily_date = ws_process_date
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    ws_daily_summary.daily_trans_amount = ws_total_trans_amount
    ws_daily_summary.daily_deposits = ws_total_deposits
    ws_daily_summary.daily_withdrawals = ws_total_withdrawals

    with open("daily_summary_file", "a") as f:
        f.write(str(ws_daily_summary) + "\n")

def weekly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs weekly data aggregation."""
    if ws_day_of_week == 7:
        global ws_weekly_summary
        ws_weekly_summary = WeeklySummary()
        ws_weekly_summary.weekly_week = ws_week_number
        sum_week_data_C10()
        with open("weekly_summary_file", "a") as f:
            f.write(str(ws_weekly_summary) + "\n")

def sum_week_data_C10() -> None:
    """Sums weekly data."""
    global weekly_trans_count, weekly_trans_amount
    weekly_trans_count = 0
    weekly_trans_amount = 0
    for _ in range(7):
        weekly_trans_count += daily_trans_count
        weekly_trans_amount += daily_trans_amount

def monthly_aggregation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs monthly data aggregation."""
    if ws_end_of_month == 'Y':
        global ws_monthly_summary
        ws_monthly_summary = MonthlySummary()
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_month = ws_curr_month
        ws_monthly_summary.monthly_year = ws_curr_year
        sum_month_data_C10()

        with open("monthly_summary_file", "a") as f:
            f.write(str(ws_monthly_summary) + "\n")

def sum_month_data_C10() -> None:
    """Sums monthly data."""
    global monthly_trans_count, monthly_trans_amount, monthly_new_accounts, monthly_closed_accounts, ws_eof_flag
    monthly_trans_count = 0
    monthly_trans_amount = 0
    monthly_new_accounts = 0
    monthly_closed_accounts = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                if daily_month(ws_daily_sum_rec) == ws_curr_month:
                    monthly_trans_count += int(daily_trans_count_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec))
                    monthly_trans_amount += Decimal(daily_trans_amount_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec))
        except FileNotFoundError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates key performance indicators (KPIs)."""
    calc_financial_kpi_C10()
    calc_operational_kpi_C10()
    calc_customer_kpi_C10()

def calc_financial_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates financial KPIs."""
    global ws_roa, ws_roe, ws_nim
    if ws_total_assets > 0:
        ws_roa = (ws_net_income / ws_total_assets) * 100
    if ws_total_equity > 0:
        ws_roe = (ws_net_income / ws_total_equity) * 100
    if ws_interest_expense > 0:
        ws_nim = ((ws_interest_income - ws_interest_expense) / ws_earning_assets) * 100

def calc_operational_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates operational KPIs."""
    global ws_error_rate, ws_sla_compliance, ws_first_call_resolution
    if ws_total_trans_count > 0:
        ws_error_rate = (ws_error_count / ws_total_trans_count) * 100
    ws_sla_compliance = (ws_within_sla_count / ws_total_cases) * 100
    ws_first_call_resolution = (ws_fcr_count / ws_total_calls) * 100

def calc_customer_kpi_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates customer KPIs."""
    global ws_churn_rate, ws_acquisition_cost, ws_lifetime_value
    if ws_active_customers > 0:
        ws_churn_rate = (ws_churned_customers / ws_active_customers) * 100
    ws_acquisition_cost = ws_marketing_spend / ws_new_customers
    ws_lifetime_value = ws_avg_revenue_per_customer * ws_avg_customer_tenure

def generate_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Generates dashboards."""
    create_executive_dashboard_C10()
    create_operations_dashboard_C10()
    create_risk_dashboard_C10()

def create_executive_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates the executive dashboard."""
    global ws_exec_dashboard
    ws_exec_dashboard = DashboardRecord()
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_title = 'EXECUTIVE DASHBOARD'
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_revenue = ws_total_revenue
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_net_income = ws_net_income
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roa = ws_roa
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_roe = ws_roe
    ws_exec_dashboard.dash_customers = ws_active_customers

    with open("dashboard_file", "a") as f:
        f.write(str(ws_exec_dashboard) + "\n")

def create_operations_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates the operations dashboard."""
    global ws_ops_dashboard
    ws_ops_dashboard = DashboardRecord()
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_title = 'OPERATIONS DASHBOARD'
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_trans_count = ws_total_trans_count
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_avg_response = ws_avg_response_time
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_error_rate = ws_error_rate
    ws_ops_dashboard.dash_sla_pct = ws_sla_compliance

    with open("dashboard_file", "a") as f:
        f.write(str(ws_ops_dashboard) + "\n")

def create_risk_dashboard_C10() -> None:
    """Creates the risk dashboard."""
    global ws_risk_dashboard
    ws_risk_dashboard = DashboardRecord()
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_title = 'RISK DASHBOARD'
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_fraud_score = ws_fraud_score
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_npl = ws_npl_ratio
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_capital = ws_capital_ratio
    ws_risk_dashboard.dash_liquidity = ws_liquidity_ratio

    with open("dashboard_file", "a") as f:
        f.write(str(ws_risk_dashboard) + "\n")

def export_data_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data."""
    export_csv_C10()
    export_xml_C10()
    export_json_C10()

def export_csv_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to CSV format."""
    global ws_eof_flag

    try:
        csv_export_file = open("csv_export_file", "w")
        ws_csv_header = 'Date,TransCount,TransAmount,Deposits,Withdrawals'
        csv_export_file.write(ws_csv_header + "\n")

        ws_eof_flag = 'N'
        while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
            try:
                ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
                if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                    ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
                else:
                    daily_date_val = daily_date_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
                    daily_trans_count_val = daily_trans_count_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
                    daily_trans_amount_val = daily_trans_amount_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
                    daily_deposits_val = daily_deposits_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
                    daily_withdrawals_val = daily_withdrawals_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)

                    ws_csv_line = f"{daily_date_val},{daily_trans_count_val},{daily_trans_amount_val},{daily_deposits_val},{daily_withdrawals_val}"
                    csv_export_file.write(ws_csv_line + "\n")
            except FileNotFoundError:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

        csv_export_file.close()
        ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error exporting to CSV: {e}")

def export_xml_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to XML format."""
    try:
        xml_export_file = open("xml_export_file", "w")
        xml_export_file.write('<?xml version="1.0"?>\n')
        xml_export_file.write('<DailySummaries>\n')
        write_xml_records_C10()
        xml_export_file.write('</DailySummaries>\n')
        xml_export_file.close()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error exporting to XML: {e}")

def write_xml_records_C10() -> None:
    """Writes XML records."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                format_xml_record_C10(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_xml_record_C10(ws_daily_sum_rec: str) -> None:
    """Formats a single record to XML format."""
    try:
        xml_export_file = open("xml_export_file", "a")

        xml_export_file.write('<Summary>\n')
        daily_date_val = daily_date_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        xml_export_file.write(f'<Date>{daily_date_val}</Date>
')

        daily_trans_count_val = daily_trans_count_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        xml_export_file.write(f'<TransCount>{daily_trans_count_val}</TransCount>
')

        xml_export_file.write('</Summary>\n')

        xml_export_file.close()

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error formatting XML record: {e}")

def export_json_C10() -> None:
    """Exports data to JSON format."""
    try:
        json_export_file = open("json_export_file", "w")
        json_export_file.write('{"dailySummaries":[\n')
        write_json_records_C10()
        json_export_file.write(']}\n')
        json_export_file.close()
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error exporting to JSON: {e}")

def write_json_records_C10() -> None:
    """Writes JSON records."""
    global ws_eof_flag, ws_first_record
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    ws_first_record = 'N'

    try:
        while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
            daily_summary_file.seek(daily_summary_file.tell())
            ws_daily_sum_rec = daily_summary_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_daily_sum_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                format_json_record_C10(ws_daily_sum_rec)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def format_json_record_C10(ws_daily_sum_rec: str) -> None:
    """Formats a single record to JSON format."""
    global ws_first_record

    try:
        json_export_file = open("json_export_file", "a")

        if ws_first_record == 'Y':
            ws_json_comma = ','
        else:
            ws_json_comma = ''
            ws_first_record = 'Y'

        daily_date_val = daily_date_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        daily_trans_count_val = daily_trans_count_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)
        daily_trans_amount_val = daily_trans_amount_from_record(ws_daily_sum_rec)

        ws_json_line = f'{ws_json_comma}{{"date":"{daily_date_val}","transCount":{daily_trans_count_val},"transAmount":{daily_trans_amount_val}}}'
        json_export_file.write(ws_json_line + '\n')

        json_export_file.close()

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"Error formatting JSON record: {e}")

def account_maintenance_C10() -> None:
    """Performs account maintenance procedures."""
    dormant_account_check_C10()
    escheatment_processing_C10()
    account_closure_C10()
    account_reactivation_C10()

def dormant_account_check_C10() -> None:
    """Checks for dormant accounts."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = account_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_account_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                check_activity_C10(ws_account_rec)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_activity_C10(ws_account_rec: str) -> None:
    """Checks account activity."""
    global ws_days_inactive
    ws_days_inactive = int(ws_process_date) - int(account_last_activity(ws_account_rec))

    if ws_days_inactive > 365:
        account_status(ws_account_rec) = 'D'
        mark_dormant_C10(ws_account_rec)

def mark_dormant_C10(ws_account_rec: str) -> None:
    """Marks an account as dormant."""
    account_status_desc(ws_account_rec) = 'DORMANT'
    account_dormant_date(ws_account_rec) = ws_process_date
    update_account_record(ws_account_rec)
    send_dormant_notice_C10()

def send_dormant_notice_C10() -> None:
    """Sends a dormant account notice."""
    ws_notif_type = 'dormant_notice'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important: Your account is dormant'
    send_notification_C10()

def escheatment_processing_C10() -> None:
    """Processes escheatment for dormant accounts."""
    global ws_eof_flag
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

    while ws_eof_flag != 'Y':
        try:
            ws_account_rec = account_file.readline().strip()
            if not ws_account_rec:
                ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
            else:
                if account_status(ws_account_rec) == 'D':
                    check_escheatment_C10(ws_account_rec)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'

    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def check_escheatment_C10(ws_account_rec: str) -> None:
    """Checks if an account is eligible for escheatment."""
    global ws_dormant_years
    ws_dormant_years = (int(ws_process_date) - int(account_dormant_date(ws_account_rec))) / 365

    if ws_dormant_years >= ws_escheat_years:
        escheat_account_C10(ws_account_rec)

def escheat_account_C10(ws_account_rec: str) -> None:
    """Escheats an account."""
    global ws_escheat_amount
    account_status(ws_account_rec) = 'E'
    ws_escheat_amount = account_balance(ws_account_rec)
    account_balance(ws_account_rec) = 0
    create_escheat_record_C10(ws_account_rec)
    update_account_record(ws_account_rec)

def create_escheat_record_C10(ws_account_rec: str) -> None:
    """Creates an escheat record."""
    global ws_escheat_record
    ws_escheat_record = EscheatRecord()
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_account = account_id(ws_account_rec)
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_amount = ws_escheat_amount
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_date = ws_process_date
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_owner = account_owner_name(ws_account_rec)
    ws_escheat_record.escheat_address = account_owner_address(ws_account_rec)

    with open("escheat_file", "a") as f:
        f.write(str(ws_escheat_record) + "\n")

def account_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes account closures."""
    if ws_close_request == 'Y':
        validate_closure_C10()
        if ws_closure_valid == 'Y':
            process_closure_C10()
        else:
            reject_closure_C10()

def validate_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Validates an account closure request."""
    global ws_closure_valid, ws_closure_reject

    ws_closure_valid = 'Y'
    if account_balance < 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'NEGATIVE BALANCE'
    if account_pending_trans > 0:
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'PENDING TRANSACTIONS'
    if account_loan_link != '':
        ws_closure_valid = 'N'
        ws_closure_reject = 'LINKED LOAN EXISTS'

def process_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Processes an account closure."""
    global ws_final_balance
    ws_final_balance = account_balance
    disburse_balance_C10()
    account_status = 'C'
    account_close_date = ws_process_date
    update_account_record(ws_account_rec)
    archive_account_C10()

def disburse_balance_C10() -> None:
    """Disburses the account balance."""
    global ws_check_record
    if ws_final_balance > 0:
        ws_check_record = CheckRecord()
        ws_check_record.check_from_account = account_id
        ws_check_record.check_amount = ws_final_balance
        ws_check_record.check_memo = 'ACCOUNT CLOSURE'
        ws_check_record.check_payee = account_owner_name

        with open("check_file", "a") as f:
            f.write(str(ws_check_record) + "\n")

def archive_account_C10() -> None:
    """Archives the account data."""
    global ws_archive_record
    ws_archive_record = ArchiveRecord()
    ws_archive_record.archive_account_data = ws_account_rec
    ws_archive_record.archive_date = ws_process_date
    ws_archive_record.archive_retention = int(ws_process_date) + 2555

    with open("archive_file", "a") as f:
        f.write(str(ws_archive_record) + "\n")

def reject_closure_C10() -> None:
    """Rejects an account closure request."""
    ws_notif_type = 'closure_reject'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Closure rejected: {ws_closure_reject}'
    send_notification_C10()

def account_reactivation_C10() -> None:
    """Processes account reactivations."""
    if ws_reactivate_request == 'Y':
        validate_reactivation_C10()
        if ws_react_valid == 'Y':
            process_reactivation_C10()

def validate_reactivation_C10() -> None:
    """Validates an account reactivation request."""
    global ws_react_valid, ws_react_reject, ws_days_since_close

    ws_react_valid = 'Y'
    if account_status == 'E':
        ws_react_valid = 'N'
        ws_react_reject = 'ACCOUNT ESCHEATED'
    if account_status == 'C':
        if ws_days_since_close > 90:
            ws_react_valid = 'N'
            ws_react_reject = 'CLOSURE PERIOD EXCEEDED'

def process_reactivation_C10() -> None:
    """Processes an account reactivation."""
    account_status = 'A'
    account_react_date = ws_process_date
    account_dormant_date = ''
    update_account_record(ws_account_rec)
    send_reactivation_confirm_C10()

def send_reactivation_confirm_C10() -> None:
    """Sends an account reactivation confirmation."""
    ws_notif_type = 'REACTIVATION'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Your account has been reactivated'
    send_notification_C10()

def card_management_C10() -> None:
    """Performs card management procedures."""
    card_issuance_C10()
    card_activation_C10()
    pin_management_C10()
    card_replacement_C10()
    card_blocking_C10()

def card_issuance_C10() -> None:
    """Performs card issuance procedures."""
    generate_card_number_C10()
    set_card_limits_C10()
    assign_network_C10()
    create_card_record_C10()

def generate_card_number_C10() -> None:
    """Generates a card number."""
    global ws_card_number, ws_card_number_temp, ws_luhn_check
    ws_card_prefix = '4'
    ws_card_bin = ws_bin_number
    ws_card_seq = int(random.random() * 999999999)
    ws_card_number_temp = f"{ws_card_prefix}{ws_card_bin}{ws_card_seq}"
    calculate_luhn_check_C10()
    ws_card_number = f"{ws_card_number_temp}{ws_luhn_check}"


def calculate_luhn_check_C10() -> None:
    """Calculates the Luhn check digit."""
    global ws_luhn_sum, ws_luhn_check
    ws_luhn_sum = 0
    for ws_luhn_idx in range(15, 0, -1):
        ws_luhn_digit = int(ws_card_number_temp[ws_luhn_idx - 1])
        if (16 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9
        ws_luhn_sum += ws_luhn_digit
    ws_luhn_check = (10 - (ws_luhn_sum % 10)) % 10

def set_card_limits_C10() -> None:
    """Sets card limits based on card type."""
    global ws_daily_limit, ws_atm_limit
    if ws_card_type == 'DEBIT':
        ws_daily_limit = 1000
        ws_atm_limit = 500
    elif ws_card_type == 'CREDIT':
        ws_daily_limit = ws_credit_line
        ws_atm_limit = ws_credit_line * 0.2
    elif ws_card_type == 'PREMIUM':
        ws_daily_limit = 10000
        ws_atm_limit = 2000
    else:
        pass

def assign_network_C10() -> None:
    """Assigns card network based on card prefix."""
    global ws_card_network
    if ws_card_prefix == '4':
        ws_card_network = 'VISA'
    elif ws_card_prefix == '5':
        ws_card_network = 'MASTERCARD'
    elif ws_card_prefix == '3':
        ws_card_network = 'AMEX'
    else:
        ws_card_network = 'DISCOVER'

def create_card_record_C10() -> None:
    """Creates a card record."""
    global ws_card_record
    ws_card_record = CardRecord()
    ws_card_record.card_number = ws_card_number
    ws_card_record.card_type = ws_card_type
    ws_card_record.card_network = ws_card_network
    ws_card_record.card_daily_limit = ws_daily_limit
    ws_card_record.card_atm_limit = ws_atm_limit
    ws_card_record.card_expiry_date = int(ws_process_date) + 1095
    ws_card_record.card_status = 'I'

    with open("card_file", "a") as f:
        f.write(str(ws_card_record) + "\n")

def card_activation_C10() -> None:
    """Performs card activation procedures."""
    if ws_activation_request == 'Y':
        verify_cardholder_C10()
        if ws_cardholder_verified == 'Y':
            activate_card_C10()
        else:
            activation_failed_C10()

def verify_cardholder_C10() -> None:
    """Verifies the cardholder's identity."""
    global ws_cardholder_verified
    ws_cardholder_verified = 'N'
    if ws_cvv_input == ws_card_cvv:
        if ws_dob_input == ws_cardholder_dob:
            if ws_ssn_last4_input == ws_cardholder_ssn_last4:
                ws_cardholder_verified = 'Y'

def activate_card_C10() -> None:
    """Activates the card."""
    card_status = 'A'
    card_activation_date = ws_process_date
    update_card_record(ws_card_record)
    ws_notif_type = 'card_activated'
    ws_notif_channel = 'SMS'
    ws_notif_body = 'Your card is now active'
    send_notification_C10()

def card_blocking_C11() -> None:
    """Block a card."""
    global CARD_STATUS, WS_BLOCK_REASON, CARD_BLOCK_REASON, WS_PROCESS_DATE, CARD_BLOCK_DATE, WS_CARD_RECORD, WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_BODY
    CARD_STATUS = 'B'
    CARD_BLOCK_REASON  = None  # TODO: was WS_BLOCK_REASON
    CARD_BLOCK_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    rewrite_card_record_from_ws_card_record_C11()
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'card_blocked'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'SMS'
    WS_NOTIF_BODY = 'Your card has been blocked: ' + WS_BLOCK_REASON
    send_notification_C11()

def wire_transfer_C11() -> None:
    """Process a wire transfer."""
    validate_wire_request_C11()
    if WS_WIRE_VALID == 'Y':
        ofac_screening_C11()
        if WS_OFAC_CLEAR == 'Y':
            process_wire_C11()
            send_confirmation_C11()
        else:
            reject_wire_C11()

def validate_wire_request_C11() -> None:
    """Validate the wire request."""
    global WS_WIRE_VALID, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_CTR_REQUIRED
    WS_WIRE_VALID = 'Y'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INVALID AMOUNT'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE:
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'INSUFFICIENT FUNDS'
    if WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT == " ":
        WS_WIRE_VALID = 'N'
        WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BENEFICIARY REQUIRED'
    if WS_WIRE_AMOUNT > 10000:
        WS_CTR_REQUIRED = 'Y'

def ofac_screening_C11() -> None:
    """COBOL logic"""
    global WS_OFAC_CLEAR, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, OFAC_SEARCH_NAME, OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE, OFAC_MATCH_FOUND, OFAC_MATCH_SCORE, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK, OFAC_SEARCH_BANK
    WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'Y'
    OFAC_SEARCH_NAME  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    ofacsrch_C11(OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE)
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'OFAC MATCH'
    OFAC_SEARCH_BANK  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK
    ofacsrch_C11(OFAC_REQUEST, OFAC_RESPONSE)
    if OFAC_MATCH_FOUND == 'Y':
        if OFAC_MATCH_SCORE >= 85:
            WS_OFAC_CLEAR = 'N'
            WS_WIRE_REJECT = 'BANK OFAC MATCH'

def process_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Process the wire transfer."""
    debit_originator_C11()
    create_wire_message_C11()
    transmit_wire_C11()
    record_wire_C11()

def debit_originator_C11() -> None:
    """Debit the originator's account."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def create_wire_message_C11() -> None:
    """Create the SWIFT wire message."""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_WIRE_REF, WS_WIRE_DATE, WS_WIRE_CURRENCY, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME, WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT, WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC, WS_PURPOSE
    initialize_ws_swift_message_C11()
    swift_msg_type = 'MT103'
    swift_txn_ref  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    swift_value_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_DATE
    swift_currency  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_CURRENCY
    swift_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    swift_ordering_cust  = None  # TODO: was WS_ORIGINATOR_NAME
    swift_ordering_acct = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    swift_benef_cust  = None  # TODO: was WS_BENEFICIARY_NAME
    swift_benef_acct = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    swift_benef_bank = WS_BENEFICIARY_BANK_BIC
    swift_remit_info  = None  # TODO: was WS_PURPOSE

def transmit_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Transmit the wire message."""
    global WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE, SWIFT_STATUS, WS_WIRE_STATUS
    swiftsend_C11(WS_SWIFT_MESSAGE, WS_SWIFT_RESPONSE)
    if SWIFT_STATUS == 'ACK':
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'SENT'
    else:
        WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'FAILED'
        reverse_debit_C11()

def record_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Record the wire transfer."""
    global WS_WIRE_RECORD, WS_WIRE_REF, WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_WIRE_STATUS, WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT, WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT, WS_PROCESS_DATE
    initialize_ws_wire_record_C11()
    wire_ref  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    wire_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    wire_status  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_STATUS
    wire_from_acct = WS_ORIGINATOR_ACCOUNT
    wire_to_acct = WS_BENEFICIARY_ACCOUNT
    wire_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    write_wire_record_from_ws_wire_record_C11()

def reverse_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Reverse the debit in case of failure."""
    global WS_WIRE_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_WIRE_FEE
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_AMOUNT
    WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_FEE
    update_account_C11()

def send_confirmation_C11() -> None:
    """Send confirmation notification."""
    global WS_NOTIF_TYPE, WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL, WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT, WS_WIRE_REF
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_confirm'
    WS_NOTIF_CHANNEL = 'EMAIL'
    WS_NOTIF_SUBJECT = 'Wire transfer ' + WS_WIRE_REF + ' completed'
    send_notification_C11()

def reject_wire_C11() -> None:
    """Reject the wire transfer."""
    global WS_WIRE_STATUS, WS_WIRE_REJECT_REC, WS_WIRE_REF, WS_WIRE_REJECT, WS_PROCESS_DATE, WS_NOTIF_TYPE
    WS_WIRE_STATUS = 'REJECTED'
    initialize_ws_wire_reject_rec_C11()
    reject_wire_ref  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REF
    reject_reason  = None  # TODO: was WS_WIRE_REJECT
    reject_date  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    write_wire_reject_record_from_ws_wire_reject_rec_C11()
    WS_NOTIF_TYPE = 'wire_rejected'
    send_notification_C11()

def ach_processing_C11() -> None:
    """Process an ACH file."""
    receive_ach_file_C11()
    validate_ach_entries_C11()
    process_ach_credits_C11()
    process_ach_debits_C11()
    generate_ach_return_C11()

def receive_ach_file_C11() -> None:
    """Receive the ACH input file."""
    global ACH_INPUT_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_HEADER, ACH_FILE_ID, ACH_CREATION_DATE, ACH_ENTRY_COUNT, WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE, WS_ACH_FILE_DATE, WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES
    open_input_ach_input_file_C11()
    read_ach_input_file_into_ws_ach_file_header_C11()
    WS_CURRENT_ACH_FILE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_FILE_ID
    WS_ACH_FILE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was ACH_CREATION_DATE
    WS_EXPECTED_ENTRIES  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ENTRY_COUNT

def validate_ach_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Validate the ACH entries."""
    global WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES, WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_VALID_ENTRIES = 0
    WS_INVALID_ENTRIES = 0
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        read_ach_input_file_into_ws_ach_entry_C11()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            validate_single_entry_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def validate_single_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Validate a single ACH entry."""
    global WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID, ACH_ROUTING, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, ACH_ACCOUNT, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_VALID_ENTRIES, WS_INVALID_ENTRIES
    WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'Y'
    if not isinstance(ACH_ROUTING, (int, float)):
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R03'
    if ACH_ACCOUNT == " ":
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
    if ACH_AMOUNT <= 0:
        WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID = 'N'
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R06'
    if WS_ACH_ENTRY_VALID == 'Y':
        WS_VALID_ENTRIES += 1
    else:
        WS_INVALID_ENTRIES += 1

def process_ach_credits_C11() -> None:
    """Process ACH credit entries."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ACH_TRANS_CODE
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        read_ach_input_file_into_ws_ach_entry_C11()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('22', '23', '32', '33'):
                apply_credit_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_credit_C11() -> None:
    """Apply a credit to an account."""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, WS_CREDITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_CREDITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        update_account_C11()
        WS_CREDITS_POSTED += 1
        WS_TOTAL_CREDITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def process_ach_debits_C11() -> None:
    """Process ACH debit entries."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, ACH_TRANS_CODE
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        read_ach_input_file_into_ws_ach_entry_C11()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            if ACH_TRANS_CODE in ('27', '28', '37', '38'):
                apply_debit_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def apply_debit_C11() -> None:
    """Apply a debit to an account."""
    global ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_SEARCH_KEY, WS_FOUND_FLAG, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE, ACH_AMOUNT, WS_DEBITS_POSTED, WS_TOTAL_DEBITS, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    WS_SEARCH_KEY  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    search_account_C11()
    if WS_FOUND_FLAG == 'Y':
        if WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE >= ACH_AMOUNT:
            WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE -= None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
            update_account_C11()
            WS_DEBITS_POSTED += 1
            WS_TOTAL_DEBITS += None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
        else:
            WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R01'
            create_return_entry_C11()
    else:
        WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE = 'R04'
        create_return_entry_C11()

def generate_ach_return_C11() -> None:
    """Generate the ACH return file."""
    if WS_RETURN_COUNT > 0:
        create_return_file_C11()

def create_return_entry_C11() -> None:
    """Create a single return entry."""
    global WS_ACH_RETURN_ENTRY, ACH_TRACE_NUMBER, WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE, ACH_AMOUNT, ACH_ACCOUNT, WS_RETURN_COUNT
    initialize_ws_ach_return_entry_C11()
    return_orig_trace  = None  # TODO: was ACH_TRACE_NUMBER
    return_code  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACH_RETURN_CODE
    return_amount  = None  # TODO: was ACH_AMOUNT
    return_account  = None  # TODO: was ACH_ACCOUNT
    WS_RETURN_COUNT += 1
    write_ach_return_record_from_ws_ach_return_entry_C11()

def create_return_file_C11() -> None:
    """Create the ACH return file."""
    global ACH_RETURN_FILE
    open_output_ach_return_file_C11()
    write_return_header_C11()
    write_return_entries_C11()
    write_return_trailer_C11()
    close_ach_return_file_C11()

def write_return_header_C11() -> None:
    """Write the return file header."""
    global WS_RETURN_HEADER, WS_OUR_ROUTING, WS_OUR_COMPANY_ID
    initialize_ws_return_header_C11()
    return_record_type = '1'
    return_priority_code = '01'
    return_immediate_dest  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_ROUTING
    return_immediate_origin  = None  # TODO: was WS_OUR_COMPANY_ID
    return_file_date = get_current_date_C11()
    write_ach_return_record_from_ws_return_header_C11()

def write_return_entries_C11() -> None:
    """Write the return entries."""
    global WS_RETURN_IDX, WS_RETURN_COUNT
    WS_RETURN_IDX = 1
    while WS_RETURN_IDX <= WS_RETURN_COUNT:
        write_ach_return_record_from_ws_return_entry_C11()
        WS_RETURN_IDX += 1

def write_return_trailer_C11() -> None:
    """Write the return file trailer."""
    global WS_RETURN_TRAILER, WS_RETURN_COUNT, WS_RETURN_TOTAL
    initialize_ws_return_trailer_C11()
    return_record_type = '9'
    return_entry_count  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_COUNT
    return_total_amount  = None  # TODO: was WS_RETURN_TOTAL
    write_ach_return_record_from_ws_return_trailer_C11()

def statement_generation_C11() -> None:
    """Generate account statements."""
    prepare_statement_data_C11()
    generate_account_summary_C11()
    generate_transaction_detail_C11()
    calculate_statement_totals_C11()
    format_statement_C11()
    deliver_statement_C11()

def prepare_statement_data_C11() -> None:
    """Prepare data for statement generation."""
    global WS_STMT_DATE, WS_STMT_START_DATE, WS_STMT_END_DATE, WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT, WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL, WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    WS_STMT_DATE = get_current_date_C11()
    WS_STMT_START_DATE = date_to_integer_C11(WS_STMT_DATE) - 30
    WS_STMT_END_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_DATE
    WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT = 0
    WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL = 0
    WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL = 0

def generate_account_summary_C11() -> None:
    """Generate account summary."""
    global WS_STMT_SUMMARY, ACCT_ID, ACCT_TYPE, ACCT_OWNER_NAME, ACCT_OWNER_ADDRESS, WS_OPENING_BALANCE, WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE
    initialize_ws_stmt_summary_C11()
    stmt_account_number  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_ID
    stmt_account_type  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_TYPE
    stmt_customer_name  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_OWNER_NAME
    stmt_customer_addr  = None  # TODO: was ACCT_OWNER_ADDRESS
    stmt_opening_bal  = None  # TODO: was WS_OPENING_BALANCE
    stmt_closing_bal  = None  # TODO: was WS_ACCOUNT_BALANCE

def generate_transaction_detail_C11() -> None:
    """Generate transaction details."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, HIST_ACCOUNT, ACCT_ID, HIST_DATE, WS_STMT_START_DATE
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        read_transaction_history_into_ws_trans_hist_rec_C11()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
            if HIST_ACCOUNT == ACCT_ID:
                if HIST_DATE >= WS_STMT_START_DATE:
                    add_transaction_line_C11()
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def add_transaction_line_C11() -> None:
    """Add a transaction line to the statement."""
    global WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT, HIST_DATE, HIST_DESC, HIST_AMOUNT, HIST_BALANCE, HIST_TYPE, WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL, WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT += 1
    stmt_trans_date  = None  # TODO: was HIST_DATE
    stmt_trans_desc  = None  # TODO: was HIST_DESC
    stmt_trans_amt  = None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT
    stmt_trans_bal  = None  # TODO: was HIST_BALANCE
    if HIST_TYPE == 'C':
        WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT
    else:
        WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL += None  # TODO: was HIST_AMOUNT

def calculate_statement_totals_C11() -> None:
    """Calculate statement totals."""
    global WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL, WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL, WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT, WS_TOTAL_DAILY_BALANCES
    stmt_total_credits = WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL
    stmt_total_debits  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    stmt_net_change = WS_STMT_CREDIT_TOTAL - WS_STMT_DEBIT_TOTAL
    stmt_trans_count  = None  # TODO: was WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT
    if WS_STMT_TRANS_COUNT > 0:
        stmt_avg_daily_bal = WS_TOTAL_DAILY_BALANCES / 30

def format_statement_C11() -> None:
    """Format the statement."""
    create_header_C11()
    create_summary_section_C11()
    create_transaction_list_C11()
    create_footer_C11()

def create_header_C11() -> None:
    """Create statement header."""
    global WS_STMT_LINE, WS_STMT_DATE
    WS_STMT_LINE = " "
    WS_STMT_LINE = 'ACCOUNT STATEMENT - ' + WS_STMT_DATE
    write_statement_record_from_ws_stmt_line_C11()
    WS_STMT_LINE = '-' * len(WS_STMT_LINE)
    write_statement_record_from_ws_stmt_line_C11()

def create_summary_section_C11() -> None:
    """Create summary section."""
    global WS_STMT_LINE
    WS_STMT_LINE = 'Account: ' + StmtAccountNumberC11.__name__
    write_statement_record_from_ws

def validate_stop_request_C12(ws_check_number: int, ws_check_already_cleared: str, ws_stop_valid: str, ws_stop_reject: str) -> None:
    """Validates a stop request."""
    ws_stop_valid = 'Y'
    if ws_check_number == 0:
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK NUMBER REQUIRED'
    if ws_check_already_cleared == 'Y':
        ws_stop_valid = 'N'
        ws_stop_reject = 'CHECK ALREADY CLEARED'

def create_stop_order_C12(acct_id: Any, ws_check_number: Any, ws_check_amount: Any, ws_payee_name: Any, ws_process_date: Any, ws_stop_record: WsStopRecordC12) -> None:
    """Creates a stop order."""
    ws_stop_record = WsStopRecordC12()
    stop_account = acct_id
    stop_check_number = ws_check_number
    stop_amount = ws_check_amount
    stop_payee = ws_payee_name
    stop_effective_date = ws_process_date
    stop_expiry_date = int(ws_process_date) + 180
    stop_status = 'A'
    write_stop_record = ws_stop_record

def apply_stop_fee_C12(ws_stop_payment_fee: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_check_number: int, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """Applies a stop fee."""
    ws_account_balance -= ws_stop_payment_fee
    update_account_C12()
    ws_notif_type = 'stop_payment'
    ws_notif_channel = 'EMAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = f'Stop payment placed on check # {ws_check_number}'
    send_notification_C12()

def safe_deposit_box_C12() -> None:
    """Safe deposit box procedures."""
    box_rental_C12()
    box_access_C12()
    box_drilling_C12()
    box_billing_C12()

def box_rental_C12(ws_rental_request: str, ws_box_available: str) -> None:
    """Box rental."""
    if ws_rental_request == 'Y':
        check_availability_C12()
        if ws_box_available == 'Y':
            assign_box_C12()
            create_rental_agreement_C12()

def check_availability_C12(ws_box_available: str, ws_box_idx: int, ws_total_boxes: int, box_status: list[str], box_size: list[str], ws_requested_size: str, ws_assigned_box: int) -> None:
    """Check availability."""
    ws_box_available = 'N'
    ws_box_idx = 1
    while ws_box_idx <= ws_total_boxes:
        if box_status[ws_box_idx] == 'A':
            if box_size[ws_box_idx] == ws_requested_size:
                ws_box_available = 'Y'
                ws_assigned_box = ws_box_idx
                break
        ws_box_idx += 1

def assign_box_C12(ws_assigned_box: int, box_status: list[str], ws_customer_id: Any, box_renter: list[Any], ws_process_date: Any, box_rental_date: list[Any]) -> None:
    """Assign box."""
    box_status[ws_assigned_box] = 'R'
    box_renter[ws_assigned_box] = ws_customer_id
    box_rental_date[ws_assigned_box] = ws_process_date

def create_rental_agreement_C12(ws_rental_agreement: WsRentalAgreementC12, ws_assigned_box: int, rental_box_number: int, ws_customer_id: Any, rental_customer: Any, ws_process_date: Any, rental_start_date: Any, ws_box_size_fee: list[Decimal], ws_requested_size: str, rental_annual_fee: Decimal) -> None:
    """Create rental agreement."""
    ws_rental_agreement = WsRentalAgreementC12()
    rental_box_number = ws_assigned_box
    rental_customer = ws_customer_id
    rental_start_date = ws_process_date
    rental_annual_fee = ws_box_size_fee[ws_requested_size]
    write_rental_record = ws_rental_agreement

def box_access_C12(ws_access_request: str, ws_renter_verified: str) -> None:
    """Box access."""
    if ws_access_request == 'Y':
        verify_renter_C12()
        if ws_renter_verified == 'Y':
            log_access_C12()
            escort_to_vault_C12()

def verify_renter_C12(ws_renter_verified: str, box_renter: list[Any], ws_box_number: int, ws_customer_id: Any, ws_id_verified: str, ws_key_verified: str) -> None:
    """Verify renter."""
    ws_renter_verified = 'N'
    if box_renter[ws_box_number] == ws_customer_id:
        if ws_id_verified == 'Y':
            if ws_key_verified == 'Y':
                ws_renter_verified = 'Y'

def log_access_C12(ws_access_log: WsAccessLogC12, ws_box_number: int, access_box_number: int, ws_customer_id: Any, access_customer: Any, ws_process_date: Any, access_date: Any, access_time: Any, access_type: str) -> None:
    """Log access."""
    ws_access_log = WsAccessLogC12()
    access_box_number = ws_box_number
    access_customer = ws_customer_id
    access_date = ws_process_date
    access_time = "CURRENT_TIME"  # Replace with actual current time function call
    access_type = 'ENTRY'
    write_access_log_record = ws_access_log

def escort_to_vault_C12(ws_display_msg: str) -> None:
    """Escort to vault."""
    ws_display_msg = 'VAULT ACCESS GRANTED'
    print(ws_display_msg)

def box_drilling_C12(ws_drilling_request: str, ws_drilling_authorized: str) -> None:
    """Box drilling."""
    if ws_drilling_request == 'Y':
        validate_drilling_auth_C12()
        if ws_drilling_authorized == 'Y':
            schedule_drilling_C12()
            notify_renter_C12()

def validate_drilling_auth_C12(ws_drilling_authorized: str, ws_rent_delinquent_months: int, ws_court_order: str, ws_deceased_renter: str, ws_executor_verified: str) -> None:
    """Validate drilling auth."""
    ws_drilling_authorized = 'N'
    if ws_rent_delinquent_months >= 12:
        ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'
    if ws_court_order == 'Y':
        ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'
    if ws_deceased_renter == 'Y':
        if ws_executor_verified == 'Y':
            ws_drilling_authorized = 'Y'

def schedule_drilling_C12(ws_drilling_record: WsDrillingRecordC12, ws_box_number: int, drill_box_number: int, ws_drilling_reason: Any, drill_reason: Any, drill_scheduled_date: int, ws_process_date: Any) -> None:
    """Schedule drilling."""
    ws_drilling_record = WsDrillingRecordC12()
    drill_box_number = ws_box_number
    drill_reason = ws_drilling_reason
    drill_scheduled_date = int(ws_process_date) + 30
    write_drilling_record = ws_drilling_record

def notify_renter_C12(ws_notif_type: str, ws_notif_channel: str, ws_notif_subject: str) -> None:
    """Notify renter."""
    ws_notif_type = 'box_drilling'
    ws_notif_channel = 'MAIL'
    ws_notif_subject = 'Important notice regarding your safe deposit box'
    send_notification_C12()

def box_billing_C12(ws_box_idx: int, ws_total_boxes: int, box_status: list[str], box_renewal_due: list[str]) -> None:
    """Box billing."""
    ws_box_idx = 1
    while ws_box_idx <= ws_total_boxes:
        if box_status[ws_box_idx] == 'R':
            if box_renewal_due[ws_box_idx] == 'Y':
                charge_annual_fee_C12()
        ws_box_idx += 1

def charge_annual_fee_C12(box_renter: list[Any], ws_box_idx: int, ws_customer_id: Any, box_annual_fee: list[Decimal], ws_fee_amount: Decimal, ws_account_balance: Decimal, box_next_renewal: list[int]) -> None:
    """Charge annual fee."""
    ws_customer_id = box_renter[ws_box_idx]
    ws_fee_amount = box_annual_fee[ws_box_idx]
    ws_account_balance -= ws_fee_amount
    update_account_C12()
    box_next_renewal[ws_box_idx] = box_next_renewal[ws_box_idx] + 10000

def merchant_services_C12() -> None:
    """Merchant services procedures."""
    process_authorization_C12()
    capture_transaction_C12()
    process_settlement_C12()
    handle_chargeback_C12()

def process_authorization_C12(ws_card_valid: str, ws_fraud_approved: str, ws_credit_available: str) -> None:
    """Process authorization."""
    validate_card_C12()
    if ws_card_valid == 'Y':
        check_fraud_score_C12()
        if ws_fraud_approved == 'Y':
            check_available_credit_C12()
            if ws_credit_available == 'Y':
                approve_auth_C12()
            else:
                decline_auth_C12()
        else:
            decline_auth_C12()
    else:
        decline_auth_C12()

def validate_card_C12(ws_card_valid: str, ws_luhn_valid: str, ws_not_expired: str, ws_cvv_valid: str) -> None:
    """Validate card."""
    ws_card_valid = 'N'
    check_luhn_C12()
    if ws_luhn_valid == 'Y':
        check_expiry_C12()
        if ws_not_expired == 'Y':
            check_cvv_C12()
            if ws_cvv_valid == 'Y':
                ws_card_valid = 'Y'

def check_luhn_C12(ws_luhn_sum: int, ws_luhn_idx: int, ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_luhn_digit: int, ws_luhn_valid: str) -> None:
    """Check Luhn."""
    ws_luhn_sum = 0
    ws_luhn_idx = 16
    while ws_luhn_idx >= 1:
        ws_luhn_digit = int(ws_auth_card_number[ws_luhn_idx-1])
        if (17 - ws_luhn_idx) % 2 == 0:
            ws_luhn_digit *= 2
            if ws_luhn_digit > 9:
                ws_luhn_digit -= 9
        ws_luhn_sum += ws_luhn_digit
        ws_luhn_idx -= 1
    if ws_luhn_sum % 10 == 0:
        ws_luhn_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_luhn_valid = 'N'

def check_expiry_C12(ws_auth_expiry_date: Any, ws_process_date: Any, ws_not_expired: str) -> None:
    """Check expiry."""
    if ws_auth_expiry_date >= ws_process_date:
        ws_not_expired = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_not_expired = 'N'

def check_cvv_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_auth_cvv: str, ws_cvv_result: str, ws_cvv_valid: str) -> None:
    """Check CVV."""
    cvvverify_C12(ws_auth_card_number, ws_auth_cvv, ws_cvv_result)
    if ws_cvv_result == 'M':
        ws_cvv_valid = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_cvv_valid = 'N'

def check_fraud_score_C12(ws_auth_request: WsAuthRequestC12, ws_fraud_response: WsFraudResponseC12, fraud_score: int, ws_fraud_approved: str, fraud_decline_code: Any, ws_auth_decline_code: Any) -> None:
    """Check fraud score."""
    fraudcheck_C12(ws_auth_request, ws_fraud_response)
    if fraud_score < 70:
        ws_fraud_approved = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_fraud_approved = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = fraud_decline_code

def check_available_credit_C12(ws_auth_card_number: str, ws_search_key: str, ws_card_account_rec: WsCardAccountRecC12, ws_available_credit: Decimal, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_credit_available: str, ws_auth_decline_code: str) -> None:
    """Check available credit."""
    ws_search_key = ws_auth_card_number
    read_card_account_file_C12()
    if ws_available_credit >= ws_auth_amount:
        ws_credit_available = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_credit_available = 'N'
        ws_auth_decline_code = '51'

def approve_auth_C12(ws_auth_response_code: str, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, ws_available_credit: Decimal) -> None:
    """Approve auth."""
    ws_auth_response_code = '00'
    generate_auth_code_C12()
    ws_available_credit -= ws_auth_amount
    record_authorization_C12()

def generate_auth_code_C12(ws_auth_code: int, ws_auth_response_auth_code: Any) -> None:
    """Generate auth code."""
    ws_auth_code = int(999999) # Replace RANDOM function with random number generator
    ws_auth_response_auth_code = ws_auth_code

def record_authorization_C12(ws_auth_record: WsAuthRecordC12, ws_auth_card_number: str, auth_rec_card: Any, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, auth_rec_amount: Decimal, ws_auth_response_auth_code: Any, auth_rec_code: Any, ws_process_date: Any, auth_rec_date: Any, auth_rec_time: Any, ws_merchant_id: Any, auth_rec_merchant: Any, auth_rec_status: str) -> None:
    """Record authorization."""
    ws_auth_record = WsAuthRecordC12()
    auth_rec_card = ws_auth_card_number
    auth_rec_amount = ws_auth_amount
    auth_rec_code = ws_auth_response_auth_code
    auth_rec_date = ws_process_date
    auth_rec_time = "CURRENT_TIME"  # Replace with actual current time function call
    auth_rec_merchant = ws_merchant_id
    auth_rec_status = 'P'
    write_auth_record = ws_auth_record

def decline_auth_C12(ws_auth_decline_code: str, ws_auth_response_code: str, ws_decline_record: WsDeclineRecordC12, ws_auth_card_number: str, decline_rec_card: Any, ws_auth_amount: Decimal, decline_rec_amount: Decimal, decline_rec_code: Any, ws_process_date: Any, decline_rec_date: Any) -> None:
    """Decline auth."""
    ws_auth_response_code = ws_auth_decline_code
    ws_decline_record = WsDeclineRecordC12()
    decline_rec_card = ws_auth_card_number
    decline_rec_amount = ws_auth_amount
    decline_rec_code = ws_auth_decline_code
    decline_rec_date = ws_process_date
    write_decline_record = ws_decline_record

def capture_transaction_C12(ws_capture_request: str, ws_auth_valid: str) -> None:
    """Capture transaction."""
    if ws_capture_request == 'Y':
        validate_auth_code_C12()
        if ws_auth_valid == 'Y':
            create_capture_record_C12()

def validate_auth_code_C12(ws_auth_valid: str, ws_capture_auth_code: Any, auth_search_key: Any, ws_auth_rec: Any, auth_rec_status: str) -> None:
    """Validate auth code."""
    ws_auth_valid = 'N'
    auth_search_key = ws_capture_auth_code
    read_auth_file_C12()
    if ws_auth_rec == "NOT FOUND":
        ws_auth_valid = 'N'
    else:
        if auth_rec_status == 'P':
            ws_auth_valid = 'Y'

def create_capture_record_C12(auth_rec_status: str, ws_auth_rec: Any, ws_capture_record: WsCaptureRecordC12, auth_rec_card: Any, capture_card: Any, ws_capture_amount: Decimal, capture_amount: Decimal, ws_capture_auth_code: Any, capture_auth_code: Any, ws_process_date: Any, capture_date: Any) -> None:
    """Create capture record."""
    auth_rec_status = 'C'
    rewrite_auth_record_C12()
    ws_capture_record = WsCaptureRecordC12()
    capture_card = auth_rec_card
    capture_amount = ws_capture_amount
    capture_auth_code = ws_capture_auth_code
    capture_date = ws_process_date
    write_capture_record = ws_capture_record

def process_settlement_C12() -> None:
    """Process settlement."""
    batch_transactions_C12()
    calculate_fees_C12()
    create_funding_record_C12()
    send_settlement_file_C12()

def batch_transactions_C12(ws_batch_total: Decimal, ws_batch_count: int, ws_eof_flag: str, capture_amount: Decimal, capture_settled: str, ws_capture_rec: Any) -> None:
    """Batch transactions."""
    ws_batch_total = Decimal(0)
    ws_batch_count = 0
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        read_capture_file_C12()
        if ws_capture_rec == "END":
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            if capture_settled == 'N':
                ws_batch_total += capture_amount
                ws_batch_count += 1
                capture_settled = 'Y'
                rewrite_capture_record_C12()
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def calculate_fees_C12(ws_batch_total: Decimal, ws_interchange_fee: Decimal, ws_assessment_fee: Decimal, ws_batch_count: int, ws_processor_fee: Decimal, ws_total_fees: Decimal) -> None:
    """Calculate fees."""
    ws_interchange_fee = ws_batch_total * Decimal('0.0175')
    ws_assessment_fee = ws_batch_total * Decimal('0.0015')
    ws_processor_fee = ws_batch_count * Decimal('0.10')
    ws_total_fees = ws_interchange_fee + ws_assessment_fee + ws_processor_fee

def create_funding_record_C12(ws_batch_total: Decimal, ws_total_fees: Decimal, ws_net_funding: Decimal, ws_funding_record: WsFundingRecordC12, ws_merchant_id: Any, funding_merchant: Any, funding_amount: Decimal, funding_fees: Decimal, funding_date: int, ws_process_date: Any) -> None:
    """Create funding record."""
    ws_net_funding = ws_batch_total - ws_total_fees
    ws_funding_record = WsFundingRecordC12()
    funding_merchant = ws_merchant_id
    funding_amount = ws_net_funding
    funding_fees = ws_total_fees
    funding_date = int(ws_process_date) + 2
    write_funding_record = ws_funding_record

def send_settlement_file_C12() -> None:
    """Send settlement file."""
    open_output_settlement_file_C12()
    write_settlement_header_C12()
    write_settlement_detail_C12()
    write_settlement_trailer_C12()
    close_settlement_file_C12()

def write_settlement_header_C12(ws_settle_header: WsSettleHeaderC12, settle_record_type: str, ws_merchant_id: Any, settle_merchant_id: Any, ws_process_date: Any, settle_date: Any) -> None:
    """Write settlement header."""
    ws_settle_header = WsSettleHeaderC12()
    settle_record_type = 'H'
    settle_merchant_id = ws_merchant_id
    settle_date = ws_process_date
    write_settlement_record = ws_settle_header

def write_settlement_detail_C12(ws_eof_flag: str, ws_capture_rec: Any, capture_settled: str, ws_settle_detail: WsSettleDetailC12, settle_record_type: str, capture_card: Any, settle_card: Any, capture_amount: Decimal, settle_amount: Decimal, capture_auth_code: Any, settle_auth_code: Any) -> None:
    """Write settlement detail."""
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag == 'N':
        read_capture_file_C12()
        if ws_capture_rec == "END":
            ws_eof_flag = 'Y'
        else:
            if capture_settled == 'Y':
                ws_settle_detail = WsSettleDetailC12()
                settle_record_type = 'D'
                settle_card = capture_card
                settle_amount = capture_amount
                settle_auth_code = capture_auth_code
                write_settlement_record = ws_settle_detail
    ws_eof_flag = 'N'

def write_settlement_trailer_C12(ws_settle_trailer: WsSettleTrailerC12, settle_record_type: str, ws_batch_count: int, settle_total_count: int, ws_batch_total: Decimal, settle_total_amount: Decimal) -> None:
    """Write settlement trailer."""
    ws_settle_trailer = WsSettleTrailerC12()
    settle_record_type = 'T'
    settle_total_count = ws_batch_count
    settle_total_amount = ws_batch_total
    write_settlement_record = ws_settle_trailer

def handle_chargeback_C12(ws_chargeback_request: str) -> None:
    """Handle chargeback."""
    if ws_chargeback_request == 'Y':
        receive_chargeback_C12()
        research_transaction_C12()
        respond_to_chargeback_C12()

def receive_chargeback_C12(ws_chargeback_record: WsChargebackRecordC12, ws_cb_card_number: str, cb_card: Any, ws_cb_amount: Decimal, cb_amount: Decimal, ws_cb_reason_code: Any, cb_reason: Any, ws_cb_case_number: Any, cb_case_id: Any, ws_process_date: Any, cb_received_date: Any, cb_status: str) -> None:
    """Receive chargeback."""
    ws_chargeback_record = WsChargebackRecordC12()
    cb_card = ws_cb_card_number
    cb_amount = ws_cb_amount
    cb_reason = ws_cb_reason_code
    cb_case_id = ws_cb_case_number
    cb_received_date = ws_process_date
    cb_status = 'RECEIVED'
    write_chargeback_record = ws_chargeback_record

def research_transaction_C12(ws_cb_auth_code: Any, auth_search_key: Any, ws_original_auth: WsOriginalAuthC12, ws_trans_found: str) -> None:
    """Research transaction."""
    auth_search_key = ws_cb_auth_code
    read_auth_file_C12()
    if ws_original_auth != "SPACES":
        ws_trans_found = 'Y'
    else:
        ws_trans_found = 'N'

def respond_to_chargeback_C12(ws_trans_found: str, ws_cb_reason_code: Any) -> None:
    """Respond to chargeback."""
    if ws_trans_found == 'Y':
        if ws_cb_reason_code == '4837':
            no_card_present_response_C12()
        elif ws_cb_reason_code == '4853':
            merchandise_response_C12()
        elif ws_cb_reason_code == '4863':
            fraud_response_C12()
        else:
            general_response_C12()
    else:
        accept_chargeback_C12()

def no_card_present_response_C12(ws_avs_match: str, ws_cvv_match: str, cb_action: str, cb_status: str) -> None:
    """No card present response."""
    if ws_avs_match == 'Y' and ws_cvv_match == 'Y':
        cb_action = 'REPRESENT'
        cb_status = 'DISPUTE'
    else:
        accept_chargeback_C12()

def merchandise_response_C12(ws_delivery_proof: str, cb_action: str, cb_status: str) -> None:
    """Merchandise response."""
    if ws_delivery_proof == 'Y':
        cb_action = 'REPRESENT'
        cb_status = 'DISPUTE'
    else:
        accept_chargeback_C12()

def fraud_response_C12(ws_3ds_verified: str, cb_action: str, cb_status: str) -> None:
    """Fraud response."""
    if ws_3ds_verified == 'Y':
        cb_action = 'REPRESENT'
        cb_status = 'DISPUTE'
    else:
        accept_chargeback_C12()

def general_response_C12(cb_action: str) -> None:
    """General response."""
    cb_action = 'ACCEPT'
    accept_chargeback_C12()

def accept_chargeback_C12(cb_status: str, ws_cb_amount: Decimal, ws_merchant_balance: Decimal, ws_cb_fee: Decimal, ws_fees_charged: Decimal) -> None:
    """Accept chargeback."""
    cb_status = 'ACCEPTED'
    ws_merchant_balance -= ws_cb_amount
    ws_fees_charged += ws_cb_fee

def date_utilities_C12() -> None:
    """Date utilities."""
    get_current_date_C12()
    calculate_business_days_C12()
    check_holiday_C12()
    format_date_C12()

def get_current_date_C12(ws_current_datetime: WsCurrentDatetimeC12, ws_curr_year: Any, ws_work_year: Any, ws_curr_month: Any, ws_work_month: Any, ws_curr_day: Any, ws_work_day: Any) -> None:
    """Get current date."""
    ws_current_datetime = "CURRENT_DATE"  # Replace with actual current date function call
    ws_work_year = ws_curr_year
    ws_work_month = ws_curr_month
    ws_work_day = ws_curr_day

def calculate_business_days_C12(ws_business_days: int, ws_start_date: Any, ws_calc_date: Any, ws_end_date: Any, ws_is_business_day: str) -> None:
    """Calculate business days."""
    ws_business_days = 0
    ws_calc_date = ws_start_date
    while ws_calc_date <= ws_end_date:
        check_if_business_day_C12()
        if ws_is_business_day == 'Y':

            pass

def treasury_management_C13() -> None:

def calculate_cash_position_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates cash position."""
    global WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT
    WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position = Decimal("0.00")
    sum_vault_cash_C13()
    sum_fed_account_C13()
    sum_correspondent_balances_C13()

def sum_vault_cash_C13() -> None:
    """Sums vault cash."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_VAULT_REC, WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, VAULT_BALANCE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        read_vault_cash_file_into_ws_vault_rec_C13()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            break
        else:
            WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position += None  # TODO: was VAULT_BALANCE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_vault_cash_file_into_ws_vault_rec_C13() -> None:
    """Reads vault cash file."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def sum_fed_account_C13() -> None:
    """Sums fed account."""
    global WS_FED_BALANCE, WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT
    read_fed_account_file_into_ws_fed_balance_C13()
    WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position += None  # TODO: was WS_FED_BALANCE

def read_fed_account_file_into_ws_fed_balance_C13() -> None:
    """Reads fed account file."""
    pass

def sum_correspondent_balances_C13() -> None:
    """Sums correspondent balances."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_CORR_REC, WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, CORR_BALANCE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

            break
        else:
            WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position += None  # TODO: was CORR_BALANCE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_correspondent_file_into_ws_corr_rec_C13() -> None:
    """Reads correspondent file."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

CORR_BALANCE: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def project_cash_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects cash flows."""
    global WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, WS_NET_POSITION
    WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_outflows = Decimal("0.00")
    project_loan_payments_C13()
    project_deposit_flows_C13()
    project_investment_maturities_C13()
    WS_NET_POSITION = WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_cash_position + WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows - WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_outflows

WS_NET_POSITION: Decimal = Decimal("0.00")

def project_loan_payments_C13() -> None:
    """Projects loan payments."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_LOAN_PMT_REC, WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, LOAN_PMT_DATE, WS_PROJECTION_DATE, LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        read_loan_schedule_file_into_ws_loan_pmt_rec_C13()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            break
        else:
            if LOAN_PMT_DATE <= WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows += None  # TODO: was LOAN_PMT_AMOUNT
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_loan_schedule_file_into_ws_loan_pmt_rec_C13() -> None:
    """Reads loan schedule file."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def project_deposit_flows_C13() -> None:
    """Projects deposit flows."""
    global WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS, WS_PROJECTION_DAYS, WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS, WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS, WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS, WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT
    WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS = WS_AVG_DAILY_DEPOSITS * WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
    WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS = WS_AVG_DAILY_WITHDRAWALS * WS_PROJECTION_DAYS
    WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows += WS_EXPECTED_DEPOSITS
    WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_outflows += WS_EXPECTED_WITHDRAWALS

def project_investment_maturities_C13() -> None:
    """Projects investment maturities."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG, WS_INV_REC, WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, INV_MATURITY_DATE, WS_PROJECTION_DATE, INV_PAR_VALUE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'
    while WS_EOF_FLAG != 'Y':
        read_investment_file_into_ws_inv_rec_C13()
        if WS_EOF_FLAG == 'Y':
            break
        else:
            if INV_MATURITY_DATE <= WS_PROJECTION_DATE:
                WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_projected_inflows += None  # TODO: was INV_PAR_VALUE
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'N'

def read_investment_file_into_ws_inv_rec_C13() -> None:
    """Reads investment file."""
    global WS_EOF_FLAG
    WS_EOF_FLAG = 'Y'

def manage_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Manages reserves."""
    global WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY
    calculate_reserve_requirement_C13()
    check_reserve_position_C13()
    if WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY == 'Y':
        cover_reserve_shortfall_C13()
    else:
        invest_excess_reserves_C13()

def calculate_reserve_requirement_C13() -> None:
    """Calculates reserve requirement."""
    global WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS, WS_RESERVE_RATIO, WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT
    WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_reserve_requirement = WS_TOTAL_DEPOSITS * WS_RESERVE_RATIO

def check_reserve_position_C13() -> None:
    """Checks reserve position."""
    global WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY
    WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves = WS_FED_BALANCE - WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_reserve_requirement
    if WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves < 0:
        WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY = 'Y'
    else:
        WS_RESERVE_DEFICIENCY = 'N'

def cover_reserve_shortfall_C13() -> None:
    """Covers reserve shortfall."""
    global WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, WS_SHORTFALL_AMOUNT
    WS_SHORTFALL_AMOUNT = Decimal("0") - WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves
    borrow_fed_funds_C13()

def borrow_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Borrows fed funds."""
    global WS_FED_FUNDS_TRANSACTION, FF_TRANS_TYPE, WS_SHORTFALL_AMOUNT, FF_AMOUNT, WS_FED_FUNDS_RATE, FF_RATE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, FF_SETTLE_DATE, FF_MATURITY_DATE
    WS_FED_FUNDS_TRANSACTION = ""
    FF_TRANS_TYPE = 'BORROW'
    FF_AMOUNT  = None  # TODO: was WS_SHORTFALL_AMOUNT
    FF_RATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_FED_FUNDS_RATE
    FF_SETTLE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    FF_MATURITY_DATE = int(datetime.strptime(WS_PROCESS_DATE, '%Y%m%d').toordinal()) + 1
    write_fed_funds_record_from_ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13()

def write_fed_funds_record_from_ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13() -> None:
    """Writes fed funds record."""
    pass

def invest_excess_reserves_C13() -> None:
    """Invests excess reserves."""
    global WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, WS_MIN_INVEST_AMOUNT
    if WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves > WS_MIN_INVEST_AMOUNT:
        sell_fed_funds_C13()

def sell_fed_funds_C13() -> None:
    """Sells fed funds."""
    global WS_FED_FUNDS_TRANSACTION, FF_TRANS_TYPE, WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT, FF_AMOUNT, WS_FED_FUNDS_RATE, FF_RATE, WS_PROCESS_DATE, FF_SETTLE_DATE, FF_MATURITY_DATE
    WS_FED_FUNDS_TRANSACTION = ""
    FF_TRANS_TYPE = 'SELL'
    FF_AMOUNT = WS_TREASURY_MANAGEMENT.ws_excess_reserves
    FF_RATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_FED_FUNDS_RATE
    FF_SETTLE_DATE  = None  # TODO: was WS_PROCESS_DATE
    FF_MATURITY_DATE = int(datetime.strptime(WS_PROCESS_DATE, '%Y%m%d').toordinal()) + 1
    write_fed_funds_record_from_ws_fed_funds_transaction_C13()

    review_investment_portfolio_C13()
    execute_investment_strategy_C13()
    mark_to_market_C13()

def update_cfp_document_C14() -> None:
    """Updates CFP document."""
    global WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE_C14, CFP_OVERALL_STATUS_C14, CFP_TOTAL_SOURCES_C14, CFP_STRESS_NEEDS_C14, WS_CFP_STATUS_C14, WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING_C14, WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS_C14
    WS_CFP_UPDATE_DATE_C14 = str(date.today())
    CFP_OVERALL_STATUS_C14  = None  # TODO: was WS_CFP_STATUS_C14
    CFP_TOTAL_SOURCES_C14 = WS_AVAILABLE_FUNDING_C14
    CFP_STRESS_NEEDS_C14 = WS_STRESSED_OUTFLOWS_C14
    #REWRITE cfp_record FROM ws_cfp_document
    pass

def capital_management_C14() -> None:
    """Capital management procedures."""
    calculate_capital_ratios_C14()
    risk_weighted_assets_C14()
    capital_planning_C14()
    stress_testing_C14()

def calculate_capital_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates capital ratios."""
    calculate_tier1_C14()
    calculate_tier2_C14()
    calculate_ratios_C14()

def calculate_tier1_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates Tier 1 capital."""
    global WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_COMMON_STOCK_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_AOCI_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= None  # TODO: was WS_GOODWILL_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= None  # TODO: was WS_INTANGIBLES_C14
    WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 -= WS_DTA_DEDUCTION_C14

def calculate_tier2_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates Tier 2 capital."""
    global WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_SUB_DEBT_C14
    WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14 += WS_ALLL_ELIGIBLE_C14
    WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14 = WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 + WS_TIER2_CAPITAL_C14

def calculate_ratios_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates capital ratios."""
    global WS_CET1_RATIO_C14, WS_CAPITAL_RATIO_C14, WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO_C14
    if WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 > 0:
        WS_CET1_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14) * 100
        WS_CAPITAL_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14) * 100
    if WS_TOTAL_ASSETS_C14 > 0:
        WS_LEVERAGE_RATIO_C14 = (WS_TIER1_CAPITAL_C14 / WS_TOTAL_ASSETS_C14) * 100

def risk_weighted_assets_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates risk-weighted assets."""
    global WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    credit_rwa_C14()
    market_rwa_C14()
    operational_rwa_C14()

def credit_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates credit risk-weighted assets."""
    global WS_CASH_RWA_C14, WS_GOVT_RWA_C14, WS_BANK_RWA_C14, WS_MORTGAGE_RWA_C14, WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA_C14, WS_CONSUMER_RWA_C14, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14
    WS_CASH_RWA_C14 = WS_CASH_POSITION_C14 * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_GOVT_RWA_C14 = WS_GOVT_SECURITIES_C14 * Decimal("0.00")
    WS_BANK_RWA_C14 = WS_BANK_DEPOSITS_C14 * Decimal("0.20")
    WS_MORTGAGE_RWA_C14 = WS_RESIDENTIAL_MORTGAGES_C14 * Decimal("0.50")
    WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA_C14 = WS_COMMERCIAL_LOANS_C14 * Decimal("1.00")
    WS_CONSUMER_RWA_C14 = WS_CONSUMER_LOANS_C14 * Decimal("1.00")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_CASH_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_GOVT_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_BANK_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_MORTGAGE_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += WS_COMMERCIAL_RWA_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_CONSUMER_RWA_C14

def market_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates market risk-weighted assets."""
    global WS_MARKET_RWA_C14, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14
    WS_MARKET_RWA_C14 = WS_TRADING_ASSETS_C14 * WS_MARKET_RISK_FACTOR_C14
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += None  # TODO: was WS_MARKET_RWA_C14

def operational_rwa_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates operational risk-weighted assets."""
    global WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA_C14, WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14
    WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA_C14 = WS_GROSS_INCOME_C14 * WS_OPERATIONAL_FACTOR_C14 * Decimal("12.5")
    WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 += WS_OPERATIONAL_RWA_C14

def capital_planning_C14() -> None:
    """Performs capital planning."""
    project_capital_needs_C14()
    identify_capital_actions_C14()
    update_capital_plan_C14()

def project_capital_needs_C14() -> None:
    """Projects capital needs."""
    global WS_PROJECTED_RWA_C14, WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL_C14, WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14
    WS_PROJECTED_RWA_C14 = WS_RISK_WEIGHTED_ASSETS_C14 * (1 + WS_GROWTH_RATE_C14)
    WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL_C14 = WS_PROJECTED_RWA_C14 * WS_TARGET_RATIO_C14 / 100
    WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 = WS_REQUIRED_CAPITAL_C14 - WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL_C14

def identify_capital_actions_C14() -> None:
    """Identifies capital actions."""
    global WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14
    if WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 > 0:
        if WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 <= WS_RETAINED_EARNINGS_PROJ_C14:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'ORGANIC GROWTH'
        elif WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14 <= WS_SUB_DEBT_CAPACITY_C14:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'SUB DEBT ISSUANCE'
        else:
            WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'EQUITY RAISE'
    else:
        WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14 = 'NO ACTION NEEDED'

def update_capital_plan_C14() -> None:
    """Updates capital plan."""
    global WS_PLAN_UPDATE_DATE_C14, PLAN_RECOMMENDED_ACTION_C14, PLAN_GAP_AMOUNT_C14, WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14, WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14
    WS_PLAN_UPDATE_DATE_C14 = str(date.today())
    PLAN_RECOMMENDED_ACTION_C14 = WS_CAPITAL_ACTION_C14
    PLAN_GAP_AMOUNT_C14  = None  # TODO: was WS_CAPITAL_GAP_C14
    #REWRITE capital_plan_record FROM ws_capital_plan
    pass

def stress_testing_C14() -> None:
    """Performs stress testing."""
    run_baseline_C14()
    run_adverse_C14()
    run_severely_adverse_C14()
    compile_results_C14()

def run_baseline_C14() -> None:
    """Runs baseline scenario."""
    global WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14, WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14, WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14, WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14, WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14
    WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14 = 'BASELINE'
    WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14 = Decimal("2.50")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14 = Decimal("4.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14 = Decimal("0.00")
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def run_adverse_C14() -> None:
    """Runs adverse scenario."""
    global WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14, WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14, WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14, WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14, WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14
    WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14 = 'ADVERSE'
    WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 = Decimal("2.00")
    WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14 = Decimal("-1.50")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14 = Decimal("7.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14 = Decimal("-15.00")
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def run_severely_adverse_C14() -> None:
    """Runs severely adverse scenario."""
    global WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14, WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14, WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14, WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14, WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14
    WS_SCENARIO_NAME_C14 = 'severely_adverse'
    WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 = Decimal("3.00")
    WS_GDP_CHANGE_C14 = Decimal("-6.00")
    WS_UNEMPLOYMENT_RATE_C14 = Decimal("10.00")
    WS_HOUSING_DECLINE_C14 = Decimal("-30.00")
    calculate_stress_impact_C14()

def compile_results_C14() -> None:
    """Compiles stress test results."""
    print('STRESS TEST RESULTS COMPILED')
    if WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14 == 'FAIL':
        remediation_actions_C14()

def calculate_stress_impact_C14() -> None:
    """Calculates stress impact."""
    global WS_CREDIT_LOSSES_C14, WS_MARKET_LOSSES_C14, WS_STRESS_LOSSES_C14, WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL_C14, WS_STRESSED_RATIO_C14, WS_STRESS_PASS_FAIL_C14, WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL2_C14
    WS_CREDIT_LOSSES_C14 = WS_LOAN_PORTFOLIO_C14 * WS_STRESS_LGD_C14 * WS_STRESS_PD_C14
    WS_MARKET_LOSSES_C14 = WS_TRADING_ASSETS_C14 * WS_RATE_SHOCK_C14 / 100
    WS_STRESS_LOSSES_C14 = WS_CREDIT_LOSSES_C14 + WS_MARKET_LOSSES_C14
    WS_STRESSED_CAPITAL_C14  = None  # TODO: was WS_TOTAL_CAPITAL

def load_ic_balances_C15() -> None:
    """Loads intercompany balances from file."""
    global ws_ic_count_C15, ws_eof_flag_C15, ws_ic_balance_C15, ws_ic_array_C15
    ws_ic_count_C15 = 0
    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'
    while ws_eof_flag_C15 != 'Y':
        # READ intercompany_file INTO ws_ic_balance
        # Assuming a function to simulate reading from a file
        ws_ic_balance_C15 = read_intercompany_file()
        if ws_ic_balance_C15 is None:  # Simulate AT END
            ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'Y'
        else:  # Simulate NOT AT END
            ws_ic_count_C15 += 1
            if len(ws_ic_array_C15) < ws_ic_count_C15:
                ws_ic_array_C15.append(ws_ic_balance_C15)
            else:
                 ws_ic_array_C15[ws_ic_count_C15-1] = ws_ic_balance_C15

    ws_eof_flag_C15 = 'N'

def match_ic_pairs_C15() -> None:
    """Matches intercompany balance pairs."""
    global ws_ic_idx_C15, ws_ic_count_C15
    ws_ic_idx_C15 = 1
    while ws_ic_idx_C15 <= ws_ic_count_C15:
        find_ic_counterpart_C15()
        ws_ic_idx_C15 += 1

def find_ic_counterpart_C15() -> None:
    """Finds counterpart for an intercompany balance."""
    global ws_ic_idx_C15, ws_search_from_C15, ws_search_to_C15, ws_ic_idx2_C15, ws_ic_count_C15
    ws_search_from_C15 = "ic_from_entity" #IC_FROM_ENTITY(ws_ic_idx_C15)
    ws_search_to_C15 = "ic_to_entity" #IC_TO_ENTITY(ws_ic_idx_C15)
    ws_ic_idx2_C15 = 1
    while ws_ic_idx2_C15 <= ws_ic_count_C15:
        if "ic_from_entity" == ws_search_to_C15: #IC_FROM_ENTITY(ws_ic_idx2_C15)
            if "ic_to_entity" == ws_search_from_C15: #IC_TO_ENTITY(ws_ic_idx2_C15)
                compute_ic_diff_C15()
                break
        ws_ic_idx2_C15 += 1

def compute_ic_diff_C15() -> None:
    """Computes intercompany difference and logs if non-zero."""
    global ws_ic_idx_C15, ws_ic_idx2_C15, ws_ic_diff_C15
    ws_ic_diff_C15 = 1 + 1 #IC_AMOUNT(ws_ic_idx_C15) + IC_AMOUNT(ws_ic_idx2_C15)
    if ws_ic_diff_C15 != Decimal('0.00'):
        log_ic_diff_C15()

def log_ic_diff_C15() -> None:
    """Logs intercompany difference."""
    global ws_ic_diff_rec_C15, ws_search_from_C15, ws_search_to_C15, ws_ic_diff_C15, icd_from_C15, icd_to_C15, icd_amount_C15
    ws_ic_diff_rec_C15 = WsIcDiffRecC15()
    icd_from_C15 = ws_search_from_C15
    icd_to_C15 = ws_search_to_C15
    icd_amount_C15 = ws_ic_diff_C15
    # WRITE ic_diff_record FROM ws_ic_diff_rec
    pass

def cpu_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects CPU metrics."""
    global ws_cpu_utilization_C15, ws_cpu_alert_C15
    ws_cpu_utilization_C15 = get_cpu_utilization()  #CALL 'GETCPU' USING ws_cpu_utilization
    if ws_cpu_utilization_C15 > 80:
        ws_cpu_alert_C15 = 'Y'

def memory_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects memory metrics."""
    global ws_memory_utilization_C15, ws_memory_alert_C15
    ws_memory_utilization_C15 = get_memory_utilization() #CALL 'GETMEM' USING ws_memory_utilization
    if ws_memory_utilization_C15 > 85:
        ws_memory_alert_C15 = 'Y'

def io_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects IO metrics."""
    global ws_io_wait_time_C15, ws_io_threshold_C15, ws_io_alert_C15
    ws_io_wait_time_C15 = get_io_wait_time()  #CALL 'GETIO' USING ws_io_wait_time
    if ws_io_wait_time_C15 > ws_io_threshold_C15:
        ws_io_alert_C15 = 'Y'

def transaction_metrics_C15() -> None:
    """Collects transaction metrics."""
    global ws_tps_C15, ws_trans_count_C15, ws_elapsed_seconds_C15, ws_avg_response_C15, ws_total_response_time_C15
    ws_tps_C15 = Decimal(ws_trans_count_C15) / Decimal(ws_elapsed_seconds_C15)
    ws_avg_response_C15 = Decimal(ws_total_response_time_C15) / Decimal(ws_trans_count_C15)

def analyze_performance_C15() -> None:
    """Analyzes performance metrics."""
    global ws_avg_response_C15, ws_response_threshold_C15, ws_perf_degraded_C15, ws_tps_C15, ws_min_tps_threshold_C15, ws_throughput_low_C15
    if ws_avg_response_C15 > ws_response_threshold_C15:
        ws_perf_degraded_C15 = 'Y'
    if ws_tps_C15 < Decimal(ws_min_tps_threshold_C15):
        ws_throughput_low_C15 = 'Y'
